//===== Ragnarok Offline: companion-party-overhaul (was companion-life) ======
//= Companions talk like people who live in this world with you: greetings
//= by time away, reactions to towns/fields/dungeons and level-ups, combat
//= callouts with RO emotes, and conversations with each other when calm.
//=
//= How it works: a timer on each player (every 1.5 s) reads the party.
//= Companions are population-engine shells (char id 95000000 and up) and
//= real party members, so readparam/strcharinfo by char id and
//= unittalk/emotion by account id reach them. The engine drops script
//= events for shells, so everything is polled from the owner's side.
//=
//= Lines: .<category>_<personality>$ arrays, {t} = the speaker's title for
//= you, {a} = another companion's name. Personalities: 0 Rookie (Novice),
//= 1 Guardian (Swordman), 2 Scholar (Mage, Soul Linker), 3 Wanderer
//= (Archer, Bard, Dancer, Gunslinger), 4 Healer (Acolyte, Priest),
//= 5 Brawler (Monk, Taekwon, Star Gladiator), 6 Rogue (Thief, Ninja),
//= 7 Merchant.
//=
//= 1.1.0: daily life (own level-ups and job changes, resting, you going
//= quiet, time of day, long sessions) here; party-chat answers and battle
//= reactions are the engine's companion strategies (app 1.5.4), in
//= db/when/<setting>/population_strategy.yml, made by tools/make_strategy.ps1.
//= 1.2.0: memories (mod store, char scope, m.c<companion char id>.*) and
//= @clstat for the Companions window (client/index.js). Growth and the
//= fallen-companion return are npc/companion_growth.txt / companion_return.txt.
//===========================================================================
-	script	CompanionLife	-1,{
	end;

OnInit:
	.mod$ = "companion-party-overhaul";
	// 3.0.0: fewer settings. Everyday chatter = greetings, world reactions, daily
	// life and the purse talk; Combat callouts carry the emotes; Look around carries
	// the scouts' patrol; Sit down when you wait carries sitting with a favourite and
	// the restless sitting.
	.chatter = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "everyday_chatter", 1);
	.greet = .chatter;
	.world = .chatter;
	.callouts = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "combat_callouts", 1);
	.emotes = .callouts;
	.banter = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "banter_minutes", 4);
	.life = .chatter;
	.mem = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "memories", 1);
	.purse = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "purse_per_level", 2);
	.purse_talk = .chatter;
	// 3.1.0: the party loot bag (setting loot_finds: 0 off, 1 low, 2 normal, 3
	// generous). A party kill can turn up one thing from that monster's own drop
	// list, at this share (per thousand) of its normal rate (L_LootTrack).
	setarray .@lsh, 0, 100, 250, 500;
	.loot_share = .@lsh[cap_value(callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "loot_finds", 1), 0, 3)];
	// Restock in town (L_Restock): only what NPC shops sell (pre-re shop lists,
	// gear left out), bought at the shop price from the party purse.
	.rs_ok$ = ",501,502,503,504,506,512,513,515,516,517,519,522,528,535,536,537,554,577,579,580,581,601,602,610,611,643,645,656,657,712,717,721,723,726,728,729,730,734,735,736,737,740,741,742,744,745,746,747,748,902,910,911,912,919,925,934,964,965,1052,1062,1065,1092,1093,1750,1751,1752,1770,1771,6094,6095,6096,6097,6098,6099,6100,6104,6108,6110,6111,6112,6113,6114,6115,7106,7140,7141,7143,7154,7170,7433,7452,7453,7454,7455,7456,7457,7482,7521,7522,7523,7524,11515,11516,13200,13201,13202,13250,13251,13252,13253,13254,";
	.rs_max = 8;	// items on one companion's list
	.look = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "look_around", 1);
	.patrol = .look;
	.idlesit = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "idle_sit", 1);
	.sitfav = .idlesit;
	.restless = .idlesit;
	// The scouts' patrol ring: 8 waypoints round you, in order (east, then
	// counter-clockwise on the map), one cell past the party spacing.
	setarray .rx, 1, 1, 0, -1, -1, -1, 0, 1;
	setarray .ry, 0, 1, 1, 1, 0, -1, -1, -1;
	// Party spacing (db/when/spacing_*): how far from you they stand, stroll and
	// sit. With none chosen the game's own follow pulls them back anyway, and
	// the strolls use the normal distance.
	.near = 4;
	if (callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "spacing_close", 0))
		.near = 2;
	else if (callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "spacing_far", 0))
		.near = 6;
	// The Companions status window (client/index.js) asks here; its Bag tab
	// asks @clbag (bags, taking items back, the party purse).
	bindatcmd "clstat", strnpcinfo(3) + "::OnCmdStat", 0, 99;
	bindatcmd "clbag", strnpcinfo(3) + "::OnCmdBag", 0, 99;
	// Roaming orders in party chat ("go hunt" / "come back"): the client hears

	.orders = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "chat_orders", 1);

	// 2.5.6: roamers don't sit down to rest (Roam as a group); see L_RoamRest.
	.roamhelp = callfunc("F_ModSetting", .mod$, "roam_together", 1);

	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@raw$); .@i++) {
		.@s$ = .@raw$[.@i];
		while (getstrlen(.@s$) > 0 && charat(.@s$, 0) == " ")
			.@s$ = delchar(.@s$, 0);
		while (getstrlen(.@s$) > 0 && charat(.@s$, getstrlen(.@s$) - 1) == " ")
			.@s$ = delchar(.@s$, getstrlen(.@s$) - 1);
		if (.@s$ != "") {
			.ttl$[.nt] = .@s$;
			.nt++;
		}
	}

		.nt = 1;

// The tick: one NPC timer for everyone, every 1500 ms. `addrid 0, 1` runs the
// tick for each player but skips anyone busy in another script (an NPC
// dialog). Before, each player had a timer of their own, and during a long
// dialog those queued up until the queue was full and were dropped ("event
// queue is full"), which stopped them for good.
OnTimer1500:
	initnpctimer;
	addrid 0, 1;
	if (getcharid(3) >= 95000000)
		end;	// an AI player, not a person
	goto OnTick;

OnPCLoginEvent:
	@cl_away = -1;
	if (CL_LAST)
		@cl_away = gettimetick(2) - CL_LAST;
	@cl_greet = .greet;
	@cl_login = gettimetick(2);
	@cl_map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	@cl_bnext = gettimetick(2) + 180 + rand(120);
	@cl_long = gettimetick(2) + 7200;	// a long-session line after 2 hours
	@cl_afk = 0;
	@cl_pcd = gettimetick(2) + 600;	// the first purse line after 10 minutes
	@cl_pgain = 0;
	@cl_wnext = gettimetick(2) + 30;
	end;

OnPCLogoutEvent:
	CL_LAST = gettimetick(2);
	if (@cl_fire$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_fire$;	// the campfire goes out with you
	if (@cl_wp$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_wp$;	// and an open Warp Portal closes
	end;

OnPCBaseLvUpEvent:
	if (!.world || gettimetick(2) < @cl_lvl_cd)
		end;
	@cl_lvl_cd = gettimetick(2) + 20;
	callsub L_Gather;
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	if (.@a >= 0)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "lvl", "", ET_CONGRATULATION;
	end;

OnPCDieEvent:
	callsub L_Gather;
	if (.mem) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
			modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".sawfall", 1);
	}
	if (!.callouts)
		end;
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
	if (.@a >= 0)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "odead", "", ET_CRY;
	end;

// Memories: your kills while they're with you, and the MVPs among them.
// (Only the kills that are yours: the server gives no script event for a
// companion's own last hit.)
OnNPCKillEvent:
	if (!@cl_n)
		end;
	.@mvp = (getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_MVPEXP) > 0);
	if (.@mvp) {
		// for the companions to talk about afterwards
		@cl_lastmvp = gettimetick(2);
		@cl_lastmvp$ = getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_NAME);
		// A gift to celebrate, now and then (a quarter of MVPs).
		if (.gifts && @cl_n && rand(100) < 25) {
			.@g = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@g >= 0)
				callsub L_Gift, .@g, "mvp";
		}
	}
	// The party purse: a share of each of your kills for every living
	// companion with you (the monster's drops go to you as always).
	if (.purse > 0) {
		.@alive = 0;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
				.@alive++;
		}
		.@add = .purse * getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_LV) * .@alive;
		if (.@add > 0 && modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) < 1000000000) {
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", .@add);
			@cl_pgain += .@add;
		}
	}
	if (!.mem)
		end;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
		// 3.0.1: counted for the Memories tab only; no more "100 monsters!" lines.
		modstore_inc("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 1);
		if (!.@mvp)
			continue;
		.@v = modstore_inc("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 1);
		if (.@v == 1) {
			// The one boss milestone left: the first, by name.
			modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_NAME);
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "mvpms", "", ET_CONGRATULATION, 1, getmonsterinfo(killedrid, MOB_NAME);
		}
	}
	end;

OnTick:
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	.@now = gettimetick(0);

	// A conversation in progress: one line per turn, 3.5 s apart.
	if (getarraysize(@cl_qa) && .@now >= @cl_qnext) {
		callsub L_SayAid, @cl_qa[0], @cl_qn$[0], @cl_qt$[0];
		@cl_said = @cl_qa[0];	// a sitter may turn to whoever spoke last
		@cl_saidt = .@now;
		if (@cl_qe[0])
			callsub L_EmoteAid, @cl_qa[0], @cl_qe[0];
		deletearray @cl_qa[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_qn$[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_qt$[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_qe[0], 1;
		@cl_qnext = .@now + 3500;
	}

	callsub L_Gather;
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	if (.@map$ != @cl_map$) {
		// Out of town: a new trip. The Loot tab marks what's found from here on as new.
		if (callsub(L_MapKind, @cl_map$) == "town" && callsub(L_MapKind, .@map$) != "town")
			modstore_set "char", "lttrip", .@sec;
		@cl_map$ = .@map$;
		@cl_mapat = .@sec + 4;	// give companions time to arrive
		@cl_names$ = "";	// tell the client their names again (L_Gather)
		@cl_cast$ = "";	// and the portraits who's who
	}
	// Warp Portal (3.3.0): it opens once the caster has had time to cast, and closes
	// when its time is up or you've gone through (a dynamic NPC stays on the old map
	// after you leave it, so it's always taken away here).
	if (@cl_wpat && .@now >= @cl_wpat)
		callsub L_WarpOpen;
	if (@cl_wp$ != "" && .@now >= @cl_wpend)
		callsub L_WarpClose;
	if (@cl_greet && .@sec > @cl_login + 90)
		@cl_greet = 0;	// nobody came back with you: no greeting later
	if (!@cl_n)
		end;

	// The colours you dyed them (every 3 s: the engine respawns them with the
	// colours it saved at recruit).
	if (.@sec >= @cl_dyenext) {
		@cl_dyenext = .@sec + 3;
		callsub L_Dyes;
	}

	// The loot bag: monsters beaten round the party (L_LootTrack).
	if (.loot_share > 0)
		callsub L_LootTrack;

	// A companion we haven't seen this session: newly hired or drafted.
	// The ones that come back with you at login are marked quietly during
	// the first 20 s.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (getd("@cl_k_" + @cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		setd "@cl_k_" + @cl_aid[.@i], 1;
		if (@cl_ready && .greet && @cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "gnew", "", ET_DELIGHT;
		if (.mem)
			callsub L_MemSeen, .@i;
	}
	if (.@sec > @cl_login + 20)
		@cl_ready = 1;

	// Login greeting, by how long you were away.
	if (@cl_greet) {
		@cl_greet = 0;
		if (@cl_away < 0 || @cl_away >= 43200)
			.@cat$ = "gdays";
		else if (@cl_away < 1800)
			.@cat$ = "gshort";
		else
			.@cat$ = "ghours";
		if (@cl_away < 0)
			.@cat$ = "ghours";	// first login with the mod: no idea, assume a rest
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0) {
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, .@cat$, "", ET_DELIGHT;
			if (@cl_n > 1 && rand(100) < 60) {
				.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
				if (.@b >= 0)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@b, .@cat$, "", 0;
			}
		}
	}

	// You in danger.
	.@ohp = Hp;
	.@hurt = 0;
	if (@cl_ohp > 0 && .@ohp < @cl_ohp) {
		.@hurt = 1;
		@cl_hurt_aid = getcharid(3);	// the last one hurt: a Healer sits by them
		@cl_hurt_t = gettimetick(2);
	}
	@cl_ohp = .@ohp;
	if (.callouts && .@hurt && .@ohp > 0 && .@ohp * 100 / MaxHp < 30 && .@sec >= @cl_olow_cd) {
		@cl_olow_cd = .@sec + 45;
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "olow", "", ET_SURPRISE;
	}

	// Each companion: falls, revivals, big hits, low HP and SP.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		.@hp = @cl_hp[.@i];
		.@mhp = @cl_mhp[.@i];
		.@prev = getd("@cl_ph_" + .@aid) - 1;	// stored +1, so -1 = not seen yet
		setd "@cl_ph_" + .@aid, .@hp + 1;
		if (.@prev < 0 || .@mhp <= 0)
			continue;
		if (.@hp < .@prev) {
			.@hurt = 1;
			@cl_hurt_aid = .@aid;
			@cl_hurt_t = .@sec;
		}
		if (.@hp <= 0) {
			if (.@prev > 0 && .mem)
				modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".fell", 1);
			if (.@prev > 0 && .callouts) {
				.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
				if (.@b >= 0)
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@b, "down", @cl_nm$[.@i], ET_CRY;
			}
			continue;
		}
		if (.@prev == 0) {
			if (.callouts)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "revive", "", ET_THANKS;
			continue;
		}
		if (!.callouts || .@hp >= .@prev)
			continue;	// the rest only while taking damage
		.@pct = .@hp * 100 / .@mhp;
		if ((.@prev - .@hp) * 100 / .@mhp >= 35 && .@sec >= getd("@cl_sc_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_sc_" + .@aid, .@sec + 60;
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "scared", "", ET_PROFUSELY_SWEAT;
		} else if (.@pct < 15) {
			callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_HELP;
		} else if (.@pct < 35 && .@sec >= getd("@cl_lo_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_lo_" + .@aid, .@sec + 45;
			if (rand(100) < 50 && .@sec >= @cl_talk)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "low", "", ET_HPTIME;
			else
				callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_HPTIME;
		}
	}
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@msp = @cl_msp[.@i];
		if (!.callouts || !@cl_incombat || .@msp <= 0 || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (@cl_sp[.@i] * 100 / .@msp >= 10 || .@sec < getd("@cl_spc_" + @cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		setd "@cl_spc_" + @cl_aid[.@i], .@sec + 120;
		if (rand(100) < 50 && .@sec >= @cl_talk)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "sp", "", ET_SPTIME;
		else
			callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, ET_SPTIME;
	}

	// Their own milestones: a new job (which comes before a level line, a
	// rebirth lowers the level), or a level-up, with a word from another.
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		.@pl = getd("@cl_lv_" + .@aid);
		.@pc = getd("@cl_cl_" + .@aid) - 1;	// stored +1: Novice is class 0
		setd "@cl_lv_" + .@aid, @cl_lv[.@i];
		setd "@cl_cl_" + .@aid, @cl_cls[.@i] + 1;
		if (!.world || .@pl <= 0 || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (.@pc >= 0 && .@pc != @cl_cls[.@i]) {
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "jobme", jobname(@cl_cls[.@i]), ET_CONGRATULATION;
			.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
			if (.@b >= 0)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@b, "lvcg", @cl_nm$[.@i], ET_CONGRATULATION;	// (ET_CLAP is no constant: it gave no emote)
		} else if (@cl_lv[.@i] > .@pl && .@sec >= getd("@cl_lvcd_" + .@aid)) {
			setd "@cl_lvcd_" + .@aid, .@sec + 30;
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "lvme", "", ET_CONGRATULATION;
			if (rand(100) < 15)
				callsub L_Gift, .@i, "lvl";
			.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@i, -1);
			if (.@b >= 0 && rand(100) < 60)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@b, "lvcg", @cl_nm$[.@i], 0;
		}
	}

	if (.life) {
		// Resting: out of a fight the engine sits a companion down below 30%
		// SP or HP and stands it up at 95% (the Battle tab's defaults).
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || @cl_mhp[.@i] <= 0)
				continue;
			.@hpp = @cl_hp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_mhp[.@i];
			.@spp = 100;
			if (@cl_msp[.@i] > 0)
				.@spp = @cl_sp[.@i] * 100 / @cl_msp[.@i];
			if (!getd("@cl_rst_" + .@aid)) {
				if (!@cl_incombat && (.@spp < 30 || .@hpp < 30)) {
					setd "@cl_rst_" + .@aid, 1;
					if (.@sec >= getd("@cl_rcd_" + .@aid) && .@sec >= @cl_talk && rand(100) < 60) {
						setd "@cl_rcd_" + .@aid, .@sec + 300;
						callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "rest", "", 0;
					}
				}
			} else if (.@spp >= 95 && .@hpp >= 95) {
				setd "@cl_rst_" + .@aid, 0;
				if (!@cl_incombat && .@sec >= @cl_talk && rand(100) < 50)
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@i, "ready", "", ET_OK;
			}
		}

		// You going quiet for 5 minutes, and coming back.
		.@idle = checkidle();
		if (!@cl_afk && .@idle >= 300) {
			@cl_afk = 1;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "afk", "", ET_QUESTION;
		} else if (@cl_afk && .@idle < 5) {
			@cl_afk = 0;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "back", "", ET_DELIGHT;
		}

		// Two hours out: someone (a healer first) suggests a break.
		if (@cl_long && .@sec >= @cl_long && !@cl_incombat) {
			@cl_long = .@sec + 7200;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "long", "", ET_SWEAT;
		}
	}

	// Into a fight, and out of it.
	if (.@hurt) {
		if (!@cl_incombat) {
			@cl_incombat = 1;
			if (.callouts && .@sec >= @cl_fight_cd && rand(100) < 35) {
				@cl_fight_cd = .@sec + 90;
				.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
				if (.@a >= 0)
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "fight", "", ET_GO;
			}
		}
		@cl_combat_until = .@sec + 10;
	} else if (@cl_incombat && .@sec > @cl_combat_until) {
		@cl_incombat = 0;
		@cl_peace = .@sec;
		if (.callouts && .@sec >= @cl_win_cd && rand(100) < 30) {
			@cl_win_cd = .@sec + 120;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "win", "", ET_OK;
		}
	}

	if (.maxpow)
		callsub L_MaxPow, .@sec;

	// Arriving somewhere.
	if (@cl_mapat && .@sec >= @cl_mapat && !@cl_incombat) {
		@cl_mapat = 0;
		if (.mem)
			callsub L_MemMap, .@map$;
		// Back in town: what the trip turned up, then the shopping lists.
		if (callsub(L_MapKind, .@map$) == "town") {
			callsub L_LootHaul;
			callsub L_Restock, 0;
		}
		if (.world && .@sec >= @cl_map_cd && rand(100) < 70) {
			.@cat$ = callsub(L_MapKind, .@map$);
			if (.@cat$ != "") {
				.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
				if (.@a >= 0) {
					@cl_map_cd = .@sec + 90;
					callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, .@cat$, "", 0;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	// Calm: a conversation, or a thought out loud when there's only one.
	// A party of four or more (you and three companions) talks more often, and
	// more as a group (L_Conversation).
	if (.banter > 0 && !@cl_incombat && !getarraysize(@cl_qa) && .@sec >= @cl_bnext && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 20) {
		@cl_bnext = .@sec + .banter * 60 * (70 + rand(61)) / 100;
		if (@cl_n >= 3)
			@cl_bnext = .@sec + .banter * 60 * (70 + rand(61)) * 60 / 10000;
		// Late at night or early in the morning, sometimes a word about the hour
		// instead, at most once an hour. 3.0.1: the game's hour when the day-night
		// mod runs its clock ($@dn_on, night 21-5), else your own clock.
		.@lh = gettime(DT_HOUR);	// the server keeps your time zone (app 1.5.5)
		.@nf = 22;
		if ($@dn_on) {
			.@lh = $@dn_min / 60;
			.@nf = 21;
		}
		.@tod$ = "";
		if (.@lh >= .@nf || .@lh < 5)
			.@tod$ = "night";
		else if (.@lh >= 6 && .@lh < 10)
			.@tod$ = "morning";
		if (.life && .@tod$ != "" && .@sec >= @cl_tod_cd && rand(100) < 40) {
			@cl_tod_cd = .@sec + 3600;
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, .@tod$, "", 0;
		} else if (.mem && rand(100) < 30 - 15 * (@cl_n >= 3) && callsub(L_Thought)) {
			// a thought out loud: lore, a small moment, an easter egg
		} else if (@cl_n >= 2) {
			// Merchants are the chatty ones: one with you starts the talk more often.
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (rand(100) < 40)
				.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 7);
			.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0 && .@b >= 0)
				callsub L_Conversation, .@a, .@b, .@map$, .@tod$;
		} else if (rand(100) < 70) {
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
			if (.@a >= 0)
				callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, "idle", "", 0;
		}
	}

	// The party purse: now and then someone (a merchant first) says how much
	// went in since the last time.
	if (.purse > 0 && .purse_talk && @cl_pgain >= 1000 && .@sec >= @cl_pcd && !@cl_incombat && !getarraysize(@cl_qa)) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 7);
		if (.@a >= 0) {
			@cl_pcd = .@sec + 900;
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "purse", "", ET_MONEY, @cl_pgain, "";
			@cl_pgain = 0;
		}
	}

	// Gifts (setting gifts): once a minute in a quiet moment, each companion here
	// has a small chance, bigger the closer it is to you (about once in 3 hours
	// for a new one, twice as often for a close one). Not in the first 10 minutes.
	if (.gifts && !@cl_incombat && !getarraysize(@cl_qa) && .@sec >= @cl_gnext && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 15 && .@sec >= @cl_login + 600) {
		@cl_gnext = .@sec + 60;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			.@bd = callsub(L_PlayerBond, .@i);
			if (.@bd > 40)
				.@bd = 40;
			if (rand(4500) < .@bd + 10) {
				callsub L_Gift, .@i, "";
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	// Campfire nights (setting campfire): the fire's tick, or after 3 minutes of you
	// standing still with no fight, they light one themselves (once per stop, and not
	// within 20 minutes of the last camp). 3.7.0: was an offer you had to say yes to.
	if (.camp) {
		if (@cl_camp)
			callsub L_CampTick, .@sec;
		else if (.@sec - @cl_still >= 180 && @cl_cofferd != @cl_still && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 30
				&& .@sec >= @cl_clast + 1200 && !getarraysize(@cl_qa)) {
			@cl_cofferd = @cl_still;
			if (callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1) >= 0)	// somebody here and standing
				callsub L_CampStart, 1;
		}
	}

	// Bonds (3.7.0, part of Memories): every minute together counts, a new tier is
	// announced, and the tier's max HP/SP bonus is kept up (6 minutes, renewed here).
	if (.mem && .@sec >= @cl_bmnext) {
		@cl_bmnext = .@sec + 60;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
			modstore_inc("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 1);
			.@bt = callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, @cl_cid[.@i]));
			if (.@bt > modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".tier", 1)) {
				modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".tier", .@bt;
				dispbottom "[Companions] Your bond with " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " grew: " + .bt_name$[.@bt] + " (" + .bt_perk$[.@bt] + ").", 0xFFCC66;
				if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
					callsub L_QueueIdx, .@i, "bondup", "", ET_THROB;
			}
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
				callsub L_BondBuff, .@i, .@bt;
		}
	}

	// Companions roaming the map (app setting Companion roaming): they hunt on
	// their own, so the strolls, patrol laps and sitting below leave them be.
	// The engine saves the choice in its table at once; read every 5 s.
	if (.@sec >= @cl_rmnext) {
		@cl_rmnext = .@sec + 5;
		callsub L_RoamRead;
		// Sent off or recalled from the window (no chat line): no rest while roaming,
		// the thresholds back once none roams.
		callsub L_RoamRest;
		// 2.5.7: the idle Hold and Sit rules (spacing_*, idle_sit) let go of a companion
		// with the harmless Entry_Queue_Apply_Delay status, as for the strolls. Every
		// roamer keeps it (8 s, renewed every 5 s), so one that is roaming is never held
		// or sat down beside you: after a server restart and with the window's Roam
		// button too, where no chat line flips the rules' own roaming switch. 2.5.9: the
		// roam rules (db/when/roam_together) asked for it too. 3.0.0: they ask for a status
		// of their own, Entry_Queue_Notify_Admission_Time_Out, that only roamers get, so a
		// stroll near you no longer looks like roaming to them.
		// 3.7.0: the Orders tab's "Alone" (CL_ROAMALONE) leaves out that second status, so
		// the group rules let them roam each on their own.
		if (.roamhelp) {
			for (.@ri = 0; .@ri < @cl_n; .@ri++) {
				if (@cl_hp[.@ri] <= 0 || !callsub(L_Roams, .@ri))
					continue;
				sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@ri];
				if (!CL_ROAMALONE)
					sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_NOTIFY_ADMISSION_TIME_OUT, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@ri];
			}
		}
		// A new companion that starts unequipped gets its starter kit.
		if (.kit)
			callsub L_KitCheck;
	}

	// Looking around: while you stand still and nothing is fighting, every
	// 12-30 s one companion strolls a few steps to another spot near you.
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	if (.@ox != @cl_ox || .@oy != @cl_oy) {
		@cl_ox = .@ox;
		@cl_oy = .@oy;
		@cl_still = .@sec;
		// 3.0.0: the engine stands a sitter up at your every step. The idle_sit rules
		// wait for this marker (Reuse_Limit_H: it only blocks one cash item companions
		// never use) to run out, so a few steps get them up once, and they sit again
		// about 8 s after you stop, not after every step.
		if (.idlesit && !@cl_incombat) {
			for (.@si = 0; .@si < @cl_n; .@si++) {
				if (@cl_hp[.@si] > 0 && !callsub(L_Roams, .@si))
					sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_H, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@si];
			}
		}
	}
	// Not one that already went to sit by its favourite; the scouts (Wanderers:
	// archers, gunslingers) twice as often, they keep checking the perimeter,
	// unless they walk their patrol laps (scout_patrol) instead.
	if (.look && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_still >= 4 && .@sec >= @cl_wnext && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		@cl_wnext = .@sec + 12 + rand(19);
		.@w = 0;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) == @cl_still || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			if (.patrol && @cl_ar[.@i] == 3)
				continue;
			.@wc[.@w] = .@i;
			.@w++;
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3) {
				.@wc[.@w] = .@i;
				.@w++;
			}
		}
		if (.@w > 0) {
			.@a = .@wc[0];
			if (.@w > 1)
				.@a = .@wc[rand(.@w)];
			callsub L_Wander, .@a, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
		}
	}

	// Scout patrol (setting scout_patrol): while you stand still and all is
	// quiet, each scout walks waypoint to waypoint round the party, a leg every
	// 3 s, then rests 15-30 s and goes round again (sometimes the other way).
	// It stops before they gather to sit (165 s) and the moment you walk on.
	if (.patrol && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_still >= 4 && .@sec - @cl_still < 165 && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] != 3 || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) == @cl_still || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			callsub L_Patrol, .@i, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
		}
	}

	// Restless sitting (setting restless_sitting): once they've sat a while,
	// every 1.5-4 minutes one gets up: it turns to look at you, at whoever
	// spoke, or about, or it moves to a new spot, then the idle_sit rule sits
	// it down again (it waits out the same marker as the strolls).
	if (.restless && .idlesit && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		if (@cl_rstill != @cl_still) {
			@cl_rstill = @cl_still;
			@cl_rnext = @cl_still + 120 + rand(91);	// the first a minute or so after they sit
		}
		if (.@sec >= @cl_rnext) {
			@cl_rnext = .@sec + 90 + rand(151);
			callsub L_Restless, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
		}
	}

	// The last step of a walk that should end facing someone (L_FaceWalk).
	// Dropped if a fight started or you moved off meanwhile.
	while (getarraysize(@cl_fa) && .@now >= @cl_ft[0]) {
		if (!@cl_incombat && @cl_fs[0] == @cl_still && unitexists(@cl_fa[0])) {
			unitstopwalk @cl_fa[0], USW_FORCE_STOP;
			unitwalk @cl_fa[0], @cl_fx[0], @cl_fy[0];
		}
		deletearray @cl_fa[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_fx[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_fy[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_ft[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_fs[0], 1;
	}

	// Sitting with their favourite (setting sit_with_favourite): a little before
	// the idle_sit rules sit them (after a minute of you standing still, three
	// for the scouts), each walks over to the one it is most comfortable with,
	// one every 1.5 s. Bonds: L_Fav.
	if (.sitfav && !@cl_camp && !@cl_incombat && .@sec - @cl_peace >= 10) {
		.@still = .@sec - @cl_still;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) == @cl_still || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
				continue;
			.@thr = 45;
			if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3)
				.@thr = 165;
			if (.@still < .@thr)
				continue;
			setd "@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@i], @cl_still;
			callsub L_SitBy, .@i, .@map$, .@ox, .@oy;
			break;
		}
	}
	// The emote once one has got there: one a tick.
	if (getarraysize(@cl_pea) && .@sec >= @cl_pet[0]) {
		if (.emotes && unitexists(@cl_pea[0]))
			emotion @cl_pee[0], @cl_pea[0];
		deletearray @cl_pea[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_pee[0], 1;
		deletearray @cl_pet[0], 1;
	}
	end;

// Roaming orders from party chat (client/index.js): "@clroam <request> on|off
// <what you said>". Runs the engine's "@companion roam" for the companions
// named in the line (all of them when none is), and one whose state it changed
// answers in its own words. Answers OK|<changed> or ERR|<why>.
OnCmdRoam:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@on = (.@atcmd_parameters$[1] == "on");
	// The Orders tab's buttons say "tab" and work with chat orders off too (3.7.0).
	if (!.orders && .@atcmd_parameters$[2] != "tab") {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|chat orders off";
		end;
	}
	if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") <= 0) {
		dispbottom "Companion roaming is off. Turn it on in the app's Settings (Population).";
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|roaming off";
		end;
	}
	// The line, padded and without punctuation, so " name " finds a whole name.
	.@said$ = " ";
	for (.@k = 2; .@k < .@atcmd_numparameters; .@k++)
		.@said$ += .@atcmd_parameters$[.@k] + " ";
	setarray .@pun$, ",", ".", "!", "?", ":", ";";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < getarraysize(.@pun$); .@k++)
		.@said$ = replacestr(.@said$, .@pun$[.@k], " ");
	callsub L_BagParty;
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	.@n = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @clb_n; .@i++) {
		if (compare(.@said$, " " + @clb_nm$[.@i] + " ")) {
			.@who$[.@n] = @clb_nm$[.@i];
			.@n++;
		}
	}
	if (!.@n) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @clb_n; .@i++)
			.@who$[.@i] = @clb_nm$[.@i];
		.@n = @clb_n;
	}
	.@arg$ = " off";
	if (.@on)
		.@arg$ = " on";
	.@c = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		if ((inarray(@cl_rm$, .@who$[.@k]) >= 0) == .@on)
			continue;	// already roaming / already following
		atcommand "@companion roam " + .@who$[.@k] + .@arg$;
		.@chg$[.@c] = .@who$[.@k];
		.@c++;
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_RoamRest;
	// One of them answers, if it's here and standing.
	.@s = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@c; .@k++) {
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@chg$[.@k]);
		if (.@ix < 0)
			continue;
		if (@cl_hp[.@ix] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@sp[.@s] = .@ix;
		.@s++;
	}
	if (.@s) {
		.@ix = .@sp[0];
		if (.@s > 1)
			.@ix = .@sp[rand(.@s)];
		if (.@on)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "roam", "", ET_GO;
		else
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "back", "", ET_SMILE;
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@c;
	end;

// Campfire nights (setting campfire, 3.0.0): "@clcamp <request> on|off|yes" from
// your party-chat lines (client/index.js): "make camp", "time to go", or yes when a
// companion offered to light one.
OnCmdCamp:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@act$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	if (!.camp) {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|campfire off";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_Gather;
	if (.@act$ == "off") {
		if (@cl_camp)
			callsub L_CampEnd, 1;
	} else if (.@act$ == "on") {
		if (!@cl_camp)
			callsub L_CampStart, 0;
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + @cl_camp;
	end;

// Warp Portal (3.3.0): "@clwarp <request>" from your party-chat line "take us back"
// (client/index.js). In a field or dungeon: a portal to your save point, and this spot
// is remembered (CL_WPM$/CL_WPX/CL_WPY). Anywhere else (towns, indoors): a portal back
// to that spot. The caster walks over and casts; the portal itself opens in the tick
// (L_WarpOpen), which is also when the gem and SP are paid.
OnCmdWarp:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	if (!.orders && .@atcmd_parameters$[1] != "tab") {
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|chat orders off";
		end;
	}
	callsub L_Gather;
	if (!@cl_n) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's nobody here to open a portal.", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|nobody";
		end;
	}
	if (@cl_wpat || @cl_wp$ != "") {
		dispbottom "[Companions] The portal is already open. Step in!", 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|open";
		end;
	}
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	.@kind$ = callsub(L_MapKind, .@m$);
	.@home = (.@kind$ == "field" || .@kind$ == "dungeon");
	// The caster: a living Acolyte-line companion who knows the skill, the one with the
	// most SP. (The engine grants a companion its whole job tree, so every Acolyte,
	// Priest and Monk has Warp Portal Lv 4.) Skill names aren't constants: 27 = AL_WARP.
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@ix = -1;
	.@lv = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if ((eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_ACOLYTE)
			continue;
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		if (!attachrid(.@aid))
			continue;
		.@k = getskilllv(27);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@k < 1)
			continue;
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_sp[.@i] <= @cl_sp[.@ix])
				continue;
		}
		.@ix = .@i;
		.@lv = .@k;
	}
	if (.@ix < 0) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "warpnone", "", ET_SWEAT;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|no caster";
		end;
	}
	if (.@home) {
		.@dm$ = getsavepoint(0);
		.@dx = getsavepoint(1);
		.@dy = getsavepoint(2);
	} else {
		// Lv 1 only reaches the save point; a memo point needs Lv 2.
		if (CL_WPM$ == "" || .@lv < 2) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpnowhere", "", ET_QUESTION;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|nowhere";
			end;
		}
		.@dm$ = CL_WPM$;
		.@dx = CL_WPX;
		.@dy = CL_WPY;
	}
	if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOWARP)) {
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpno", "", ET_SWEAT;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|nowarp";
		end;
	}
	if (@cl_sp[.@ix] < 38 - 3 * .@lv) {
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpsp", "", ET_SWEAT;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|sp";
		end;
	}
	if (.warp_gem) {
		if (callsub(L_CampFind, "717") == "") {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpgem", "", ET_SWEAT;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|gem";
			end;
		}
	}
	@cl_wpc = @cl_aid[.@ix];
	@cl_wpcn$ = @cl_nm$[.@ix];
	@cl_wplv = .@lv;
	@cl_wphome = .@home;
	@cl_wpfm$ = .@m$;
	@cl_wpfx = .@x;
	@cl_wpfy = .@y;
	@cl_wpdm$ = .@dm$;
	@cl_wpdx = .@dx;
	@cl_wpdy = .@dy;
	@cl_wpat = gettimetick(0) + 1500;	// a walk over and the 1 s cast
	stand @cl_nm$[.@ix];	// a sitter can't walk (campfire lesson)
	callsub L_GiftWalk, .@ix;
	if (.@home)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpgo", "", ET_GO;
	else
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpback", "", ET_GO;
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + @cl_wpcn$;
	end;

// The portal opens: checked again (you may have left, the caster may have fallen or
// spent the SP), paid, and placed next to you (CpoWarpPortal, below; only you see it).
L_WarpOpen:
	@cl_wpat = 0;
	if (strcharinfo(3) != @cl_wpfm$)
		return;	// you left before it opened
	.@ix = inarray(@cl_aid[0], @cl_wpc);
	if (.@ix < 0) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] The portal fizzled: " + @cl_wpcn$ + " couldn't finish the cast.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_hp[.@ix] <= 0) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] The portal fizzled: " + @cl_wpcn$ + " couldn't finish the cast.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	.@cost = 38 - 3 * @cl_wplv;	// 35/32/29/26 SP at Lv 1-4
	if (@cl_sp[.@ix] < .@cost) {
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpsp", "", ET_SWEAT;
		return;
	}
	.@gem$ = "";
	if (.warp_gem) {
		.@gem$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "717");
		if (.@gem$ == "") {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "warpgem", "", ET_SWEAT;
			return;
		}
	}
	@cl_wp$ = duplicate_dynamic("CpoWarpPortal");
	if (@cl_wp$ == "") {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's no room for a portal here.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	// A floating source NPC is the invisible JT_FAKENPC, and so is its copy (never sent
	// to the client). 111 = the hidden NPC: the client knows it but draws no body, so
	// it carries the skill's own look, EF_PORTAL2 (what the client draws for a real
	// UNT_WARPPORTAL: a turning blue ring, looping, and light columns for 25 s). Both
	// are attached to the NPC and go with it when the tick unloads it. 3.3.1: was 45,
	// the map's ordinary portal.
	setnpcdisplay @cl_wp$, "Warp Portal", 111;
	specialeffect EF_PORTAL2, AREA, @cl_wp$;
	if (.@gem$ != "")
		callsub L_CampUse, .@gem$;
	heal 0, -.@cost, @cl_cid[.@ix];
	@cl_wpend = gettimetick(0) + @cl_wplv * 5000;	// 5/10/15/20 s, as the skill
	if (@cl_wphome) {
		CL_WPM$ = @cl_wpfm$;
		CL_WPX = @cl_wpfx;
		CL_WPY = @cl_wpfy;
		.@to$ = callsub(L_TownName, @cl_wpdm$);
		if (.@to$ == "")
			.@to$ = "your save point";
	} else
		.@to$ = "where you left off";
	.@paid$ = "";
	if (.@gem$ != "")
		.@paid$ = ", one Blue Gemstone";
	dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_wpcn$ + " opened a Warp Portal to " + .@to$ + " (" + .@cost + " SP" + .@paid$ + "). Step in within " + (@cl_wplv * 5) + " seconds.", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

L_WarpClose:
	if (@cl_wp$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_wp$;
	@cl_wp$ = "";
	@cl_wpend = 0;
	return;

// The Orders tab (3.7.0, client/index.js): "@clorder <request> <action>".
//   state     OK|companions here|portal caster or ""|why no portal or ""|portal home (1)
//             or back (0)|appraiser or ""|why no appraisal or ""|unidentified items|
//             camp lit|roamers|roam alone|roaming on (app setting)|campfire on
//   appraise  the Merchant-line companion with the most SP identifies your items,
//             10 SP each (Item Appraisal); fewer if its SP runs out
//   group / alone   the roam style, kept on your character (CL_ROAMALONE)
OnCmdOrder:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@act$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	callsub L_Gather;
	if (.@act$ == "group" || .@act$ == "alone") {
		CL_ROAMALONE = (.@act$ == "alone");
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + CL_ROAMALONE;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "appraise") {
		.@ix = callsub(L_Appraiser);
		if (.@ix < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|No Merchant in the party.";
			end;
		}
		.@n = identifyall(false);
		if (.@n <= 0) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appnone", "", ET_QUESTION;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Nothing to appraise.";
			end;
		}
		.@can = @cl_sp[.@ix] / 10;
		if (.@can <= 0) {
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appnosp", "", ET_SWEAT;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + @cl_nm$[.@ix] + " is out of SP.";
			end;
		}
		stand @cl_nm$[.@ix];	// a sitter can't walk
		callsub L_GiftWalk, .@ix;
		if (.@can >= .@n) {
			identifyall(true);
			.@done = .@n;
		} else {
			// Not enough SP for all: one by one, each given back identified (cards,
			// options, refine and binding kept, as the Bag tab's take does).
			getinventorylist;
			.@done = 0;
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < @inventorylist_count && .@done < .@can; .@j++) {
				if (@inventorylist_identify[.@j])
					continue;
				.@id = @inventorylist_id[.@j];
				.@amt = @inventorylist_amount[.@j];
				setarray .@oid, @inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id3[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id5[.@j];
				setarray .@oval, @inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value3[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value5[.@j];
				setarray .@opar, @inventorylist_option_parameter1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter3[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter5[.@j];
				delitemidx @inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@amt;
				if (@inventorylist_bound[.@j])
					getitembound3 .@id, .@amt, 1, @inventorylist_refine[.@j], @inventorylist_attribute[.@j], @inventorylist_card1[.@j], @inventorylist_card2[.@j], @inventorylist_card3[.@j], @inventorylist_card4[.@j], @inventorylist_bound[.@j], .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
				else
					getitem3 .@id, .@amt, 1, @inventorylist_refine[.@j], @inventorylist_attribute[.@j], @inventorylist_card1[.@j], @inventorylist_card2[.@j], @inventorylist_card3[.@j], @inventorylist_card4[.@j], .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
				.@done++;
			}
		}
		heal 0, -(.@done * 10), @cl_cid[.@ix];
		.@left = .@n - .@done;
		if (.@left > 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appnosp", "", ET_SWEAT;
		else
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@ix, "appraise", "", ET_OK;
		.@msg$ = @cl_nm$[.@ix] + " appraised " + .@done + " item" + (.@done == 1 ? "" : "s") + " (" + (.@done * 10) + " SP).";
		if (.@left > 0)
			.@msg$ += " " + .@left + " still unknown: ask again when their SP is back.";
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + .@msg$, 0xFFCC66;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	// state
	.@alive = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			.@alive++;
	}
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	.@kind$ = callsub(L_MapKind, .@m$);
	.@home = (.@kind$ == "field" || .@kind$ == "dungeon");
	// The portal: the same checks as OnCmdWarp, said in a few words.
	.@wc$ = "";
	.@why$ = "";
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@wix = -1;
	.@wlv = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if ((eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_ACOLYTE)
			continue;
		if (!attachrid(@cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		.@k = getskilllv(27);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@k < 1)
			continue;
		if (.@wix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_sp[.@i] <= @cl_sp[.@wix])
				continue;
		}
		.@wix = .@i;
		.@wlv = .@k;
	}
	if (.@wix < 0)
		.@why$ = "No Acolyte in the party";
	else {
		.@wc$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@wix]);
		if (@cl_wpat || @cl_wp$ != "")
			.@why$ = "The portal is open";
		else if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOWARP))
			.@why$ = "No portals on this map";
		else if (!.@home && (CL_WPM$ == "" || .@wlv < 2))
			.@why$ = "Nowhere to go back to yet";
		else if (@cl_sp[.@wix] < 38 - 3 * .@wlv)
			.@why$ = @cl_nm$[.@wix] + " needs SP";
		else if (.warp_gem) {
			if (callsub(L_CampFind, "717") == "")
				.@why$ = "No Blue Gemstone";
		}
	}
	// The appraiser.
	.@ac$ = "";
	.@awhy$ = "";
	.@un = identifyall(false);
	.@aix = callsub(L_Appraiser);
	if (.@aix < 0)
		.@awhy$ = "No Merchant in the party";
	else {
		.@ac$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@aix]);
		if (.@un <= 0)
			.@awhy$ = "Nothing to appraise";
		else if (@cl_sp[.@aix] < 10)
			.@awhy$ = @cl_nm$[.@aix] + " needs SP";
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@alive + "|" + .@wc$ + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@why$) + "|" + .@home + "|" + .@ac$ + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@awhy$)
		+ "|" + max(0, .@un) + "|" + @cl_camp + "|" + getarraysize(@cl_rm$) + "|" + CL_ROAMALONE
		+ "|" + (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") > 0) + "|" + .camp;
	end;

// The living Merchant-line companion here who knows Item Appraisal (40), the one with
// the most SP; -1 if none.
L_Appraiser:
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@ix = -1;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if ((eaclass(@cl_cls[.@i]) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_MERCHANT)
			continue;
		if (!attachrid(@cl_aid[.@i]))
			continue;
		.@k = getskilllv(40);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@k < 1)
			continue;
		if (.@ix >= 0) {
			if (@cl_sp[.@i] <= @cl_sp[.@ix])
				continue;
		}
		.@ix = .@i;
	}
	return .@ix;

// Maximize Power (3.4.0): the engine turns it on but never off, and it drains SP and stops
// SP regen while on. Off once no monster is within 9 cells of the smith for two ticks
// (about 3 s), or nobody in the party was hit for 20 s (a fight's @cl_combat_until is
// the last hit + 10; not in the first 20 s after it went on, before the first hit), or
// under 25% SP. party_roles' ct_maxpow plan bans the engine's recast until monsters
// attack again and SP is back to 40%. getarg(0) = now (s). @cl_mpon_<aid> = when seen on.
L_MaxPow:
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !getstatus(SC_MAXIMIZEPOWER, 0, @cl_cid[.@i])) {
			setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, 0;
			setd "@cl_mpon_" + .@aid, 0;
			continue;
		}
		.@on = getd("@cl_mpon_" + .@aid);
		if (!.@on) {
			.@on = getarg(0);
			setd "@cl_mpon_" + .@aid, .@on;
		}
		.@off = (@cl_msp[.@i] > 0 && @cl_sp[.@i] * 100 < @cl_msp[.@i] * 25);
		if (!.@off && getarg(0) - .@on >= 20 && getarg(0) > @cl_combat_until + 10)
			.@off = 1;
		if (!.@off) {
			if (getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0)
				continue;
			if (getareaunits(BL_MOB, .@m$, .@x - 9, .@y - 9, .@x + 9, .@y + 9) > 0) {
				setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, 0;
				continue;
			}
			.@q = getd("@cl_mpq_" + .@aid) + 1;
			setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, .@q;
			if (.@q < 2)
				continue;
		}
		sc_end SC_MAXIMIZEPOWER, .@aid;
		setd "@cl_mpq_" + .@aid, 0;
		setd "@cl_mpon_" + .@aid, 0;
	}
	return;

// Light the fire: a bonfire next to you (a dynamic NPC: only you see it, and it
// goes when you leave the map), roamers called back, everyone to a seat round it.
L_CampStart:
	if (!@cl_n) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's nobody here to share a fire with.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	if (@cl_incombat) {
		dispbottom "[Companions] Not while we're fighting!", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(@cl_rm$); .@i++)
		atcommand "@companion roam " + @cl_rm$[.@i] + " off";
	callsub L_RoamRead;
	callsub L_RoamRest;
	if (@cl_fire$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_fire$;
	@cl_fire$ = duplicate_dynamic("CampFire");
	if (@cl_fire$ == "") {
		dispbottom "[Companions] There's no room for a fire here.", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	// A floating source NPC ("-") is always the invisible JT_FAKENPC in rAthena, and the
	// copy inherits its class, so give the copy its bonfire look here (mod id 19420 =
	// 4_BONFIRE, System/jobname.lub).
	setnpcdisplay @cl_fire$, "Campfire", 19420;
	getmapxy(.@fm$, .@fx, .@fy, BL_NPC, @cl_fire$);
	@cl_cfm$ = .@fm$;
	@cl_cfx = .@fx;
	@cl_cfy = .@fy;
	@cl_camp = 1;
	@cl_cout = 0;
	@cl_cstart = gettimetick(2);
	@cl_cmeal = 0;
	@cl_crest = 0;
	@cl_cnext = @cl_cstart + 15;
	@cl_cact = @cl_cstart + 35;
	// Seats round the fire for everyone; scouts stand watch at theirs for two minutes
	// before they sit (their marker waits, L_CampTick). Riders get off: a mounted
	// character can't be drawn sitting (Brizion on his Peco looked like he stood), and
	// they get back on in L_CampEnd (the engine remounts them on a map change anyway).
	@cl_crid$ = ";";
	callsub L_CampSeat;
	@cl_cseatst = @cl_still;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0 && checkriding(@cl_cid[.@i])) {
			setriding 0, @cl_cid[.@i];
			@cl_crid$ += @cl_aid[.@i] + ":" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ";";
		}
	}
	// getarg(0) 1: lit on their own while you stood still (3.7.0).
	.@auto = getarg(0, 0);
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 7);
	if (.@a >= 0)
		callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, (.@auto ? "campauto" : "camplit"), "", ET_DELIGHT;
	if (.@auto)
		dispbottom "[Companions] You stood still a while, so they made camp. Walk away, say \"time to go\" in party chat, or click the fire to put it out.", 0xFFCC66;
	else
		dispbottom "[Companions] Campfire lit. Say \"time to go\" in party chat (or click the fire) to put it out.", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

// Seats in an even circle: the 16 cells two out from the fire, in clockwise order.
// You hold one place (the ring cell in your direction from the fire); the companions
// are spread at equal steps round the rest, so with five of them and you it's six
// evenly spaced places. A blocked or taken seat shifts to the next free cell. Each seat
// then goes to the nearest companion still without one (short walks), who walks up to
// end facing the fire. Seats handed out are kept in @cl_cseat$ (";x,y;x,y;").
L_CampSeat:
	.@fm$ = @cl_cfm$;
	.@fx = @cl_cfx;
	.@fy = @cl_cfy;
	setarray .@sx, 2, 2, 2, 1, 0, -1, -2, -2, -2, -2, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 2;
	setarray .@sy, 0, 1, 2, 2, 2, 2, 2, 1, 0, -1, -2, -2, -2, -2, -2, -1;
	setarray .@sl, 200, 224, 283, 224, 200, 224, 283, 224, 200, 224, 283, 224, 200, 224, 283, 224;	// length x100
	// Who sits: the living companions on this map.
	.@m = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@fm$)
			continue;
		.@ci[.@m] = .@i;
		.@cx_[.@m] = .@cx;
		.@cy_[.@m] = .@cy;
		.@m++;
	}
	if (!.@m)
		return;
	// Your place on the ring: the seat pointing most your way from the fire.
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	.@dx = .@ox - .@fx;
	.@dy = .@oy - .@fy;
	.@p = 0;
	if (.@dx != 0 || .@dy != 0) {
		.@bs = -99999;
		for (.@s = 0; .@s < 16; .@s++) {
			.@sc = (.@sx[.@s] * .@dx + .@sy[.@s] * .@dy) * 1000 / .@sl[.@s];
			if (.@sc > .@bs) {
				.@bs = .@sc;
				.@p = .@s;
			}
		}
	}
	@cl_cseat$ = ";" + (.@fx + .@sx[.@p]) + "," + (.@fy + .@sy[.@p]) + ";";	// yours
	setarray .@try, 0, 1, -1, 2, -2, 3, -3;
	for (.@k = 1; .@k <= .@m; .@k++) {
		.@t = (.@p + (.@k * 16 + (.@m + 1) / 2) / (.@m + 1)) % 16;
		// The seat itself, or the nearest free cell beside it on the ring.
		.@seat = -1;
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < 7 && .@seat < 0; .@j++) {
			.@s = (.@t + .@try[.@j] + 16) % 16;
			.@gx = .@fx + .@sx[.@s];
			.@gy = .@fy + .@sy[.@s];
			if (.@gx == .@ox && .@gy == .@oy)
				continue;
			if (compare(@cl_cseat$, ";" + .@gx + "," + .@gy + ";"))
				continue;
			if (!checkcell(.@fm$, .@gx, .@gy, cell_chkpass))
				continue;
			.@seat = .@s;
		}
		if (.@seat < 0)
			continue;
		.@gx = .@fx + .@sx[.@seat];
		.@gy = .@fy + .@sy[.@seat];
		// The nearest companion still standing about.
		.@w = -1;
		.@bd = 999;
		for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@m; .@q++) {
			if (.@got[.@q])
				continue;
			.@d = max(.@cx_[.@q] - .@gx, .@gx - .@cx_[.@q], .@cy_[.@q] - .@gy, .@gy - .@cy_[.@q]);
			if (.@d < .@bd) {
				.@bd = .@d;
				.@w = .@q;
			}
		}
		if (.@w < 0)
			break;
		.@got[.@w] = 1;
		@cl_cseat$ += .@gx + "," + .@gy + ";";
		.@i = .@ci[.@w];
		// One already sitting (they sit after a minute of waiting) can't walk: up first,
		// or it stayed where it sat, on the fire itself sometimes.
		stand @cl_nm$[.@i];
		callsub L_FaceWalk, @cl_aid[.@i], .@fm$, .@cx_[.@w], .@cy_[.@w], .@gx, .@gy, .@fx, .@fy, 6000;
	}
	return;

// Put it out. getarg(0): 1 when you said so (someone answers), 0 when you walked
// off or left the map. Long enough by the fire: Well Rested for everyone.
L_CampEnd:
	if (@cl_fire$ != "")
		unloadnpc @cl_fire$;
	@cl_fire$ = "";
	@cl_camp = 0;
	@cl_clast = gettimetick(2);
	// The riders back on their mounts.
	if (@cl_crid$ != "" && @cl_crid$ != ";") {
		explode(.@rd$, @cl_crid$, ";");
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@rd$); .@i++) {
			if (.@rd$[.@i] == "")
				continue;
			explode(.@ac$, .@rd$[.@i], ":");
			if (isloggedin(atoi(.@ac$[0]), atoi(.@ac$[1])))
				setriding 1, atoi(.@ac$[1]);
		}
	}
	@cl_crid$ = "";
	.@min = (gettimetick(2) - @cl_cstart) / 60;
	.@dur = 0;
	if (.@min >= 15)
		.@dur = 3600;
	else if (.@min >= 5)
		.@dur = 1800;
	if (.@dur) {
		// A camp of 5+ minutes is a night to remember (Bonds, 3.7.0).
		if (.mem) {
			for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
				modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".camps", 1);
		}
		bonus_script "bonus2 bExpAddRace,RC_ALL,10; bonus bHPrecovRate,30; bonus bSPrecovRate,30;", .@dur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, EFST_CASH_PLUSEXP;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
				bonus_script "bonus bHPrecovRate,30; bonus bSPrecovRate,30;", .@dur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, EFST_CASH_PLUSEXP, @cl_cid[.@i];
		}
		dispbottom "[Companions] Well Rested: +10% base EXP and faster HP and SP recovery for " + (.@dur / 60) + " minutes.", 0xFFCC66;
	}
	if (getarg(0)) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, .@a, "campout", "", ET_OK;
	}
	return;

// Every tick while the fire burns.
L_CampTick:
	.@sec = getarg(0);
	// You chose "Put out the fire" at the fire (CampFire marks it; the dialog is shut by
	// now, since this tick skips anyone in a dialog).
	if (@cl_cout) {
		@cl_cout = 0;
		callsub L_CampEnd, 1;
		return;
	}
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	if (.@om$ != @cl_cfm$ || max(.@ox - @cl_cfx, @cl_cfx - .@ox, .@oy - @cl_cfy, @cl_cfy - .@oy) > 12) {
		callsub L_CampEnd, 0;
		return;
	}
	// You moved about the camp: the game's follow pulled them into formation behind you,
	// off their seats. Two seconds after you settle, a fresh even ring from where you
	// stand now.
	if (@cl_cseatst != @cl_still && .@sec - @cl_still >= 2 && !@cl_incombat) {
		@cl_cseatst = @cl_still;
		callsub L_CampSeat;
	}
	// The marker that sits them round the fire (db/when/campfire, Reuse_Limit_G: it
	// only blocks one cash item companions never use), renewed every 4 s. The scouts
	// stand watch at their seats for two minutes, then sit too. (No effect
	// on the fire: EF_TORCH keeps burning once started, and repeating it stacked into
	// a huge glow; the bonfire sprite is the fire.)
	if (.@sec >= @cl_cmk) {
		@cl_cmk = .@sec + 4;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
				continue;
			// Scouts too: their two-minute watch, held at their seat, is in
			// db/when/campfire (ct_camp_watch).
			sc_start SC_REUSE_LIMIT_G, 8000, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, @cl_aid[.@i];
		}
	}
	if (@cl_incombat)
		return;
	if (!@cl_cmeal && .@sec - @cl_cstart >= 60) {
		@cl_cmeal = 1;
		callsub L_CampCook;
		return;
	}
	.@min = (.@sec - @cl_cstart) / 60;
	if (@cl_crest < 1 && .@min >= 5) {
		@cl_crest = 1;
		dispbottom "[Companions] The fire's warmth sinks in. You'll leave Well Rested (30 minutes).", 0xFFCC66;
	} else if (@cl_crest < 2 && .@min >= 15) {
		@cl_crest = 2;
		dispbottom "[Companions] A proper rest. You'll leave Well Rested (1 hour).", 0xFFCC66;
	}
	if (getarraysize(@cl_qa))
		return;
	if (.@sec >= @cl_cnext) {
		@cl_cnext = .@sec + 40 + rand(15);
		callsub L_CampTalk;
		return;
	}
	if (.@sec >= @cl_cact) {
		@cl_cact = .@sec + 50 + rand(40);
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, "campact", "", 0;
	}
	return;

// A talk by the fire: two of them, sometimes three, at night sometimes the night
// ones; one companion alone muses. Bonds grow twice as fast here.
L_CampTalk:
	if (@cl_n < 2) {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueIdx, .@a, "campsolo", "", 0;
		return;
	}
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	.@b = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
	if (.@a < 0 || .@b < 0)
		return;
	.@c = -1;
	if (@cl_n >= 3 && rand(100) < 30) {
		.@x = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
		if (.@x >= 0 && .@x != .@b)
			.@c = .@x;
	}
	.@lh = gettime(DT_HOUR);
	if ($@dn_on)
		.@lh = $@dn_min / 60;	// the game's hour, with the day-night mod
	.@cat$ = "camp";
	if (.@c >= 0)
		.@cat$ = "camp3";
	else if ((.@lh >= 21 || .@lh < 5) && rand(100) < 40)
		.@cat$ = "campn";
	.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, .@cat$);
	if (.@line$ == "")
		return;
	callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@b;
	callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@b;
	if (.@c >= 0) {
		callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@c;
		callsub L_Bump, .@b, .@c;
	}
	callsub L_QueueConv, .@a, .@b, .@c, .@line$, "", "", -1;
	return;

// The meal: the cook (a Merchant, else a Healer, else anyone) uses what's in the
// bags, theirs first, then yours. A buff for everyone, 30 minutes.
L_CampCook:
	.@k = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 7);
	if (.@k >= 0 && @cl_ar[.@k] != 7) {
		.@h = callsub(L_Pick, -1, 4);
		if (.@h >= 0 && @cl_ar[.@h] == 4)
			.@k = .@h;
	}
	if (.@k < 0)
		return;
	.@meal = 0;
	.@m$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "517,12102");
	.@v$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "515,516,535,581,622");
	if (.@m$ != "" && .@v$ != "") {
		.@meal = 1;
		.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@m$) + " and " + callsub(L_CampUse, .@v$);
	} else {
		.@f$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "579,544");
		if (.@f$ != "") {
			.@meal = 2;
			.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@f$);
		} else {
			.@r$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "512,513,514,578,568");
			.@w$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "518,519,548");
			if (.@r$ != "" && .@w$ != "") {
				.@meal = 3;
				.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@r$) + " and " + callsub(L_CampUse, .@w$);
			} else {
				.@any$ = callsub(L_CampFind, "517,12102,515,516,535,581,622,579,544,512,513,514,578,568,518,519,548");
				if (.@any$ != "") {
					.@meal = 4;
					.@used$ = callsub(L_CampUse, .@any$);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if (!.@meal) {
		callsub L_QueueIdx, .@k, "campnofood", "", ET_SWEAT;
		return;
	}
	setarray .@mn$, "", "Hunter's Stew", "Grilled Fish", "Sweet Tart", "Simple Soup";
	setarray .@bs$, "", "bonus bBaseAtk,15; bonus bHit,10;", "bonus bMaxHPrate,8; bonus bHPrecovRate,20;",
		"bonus bMaxSPrate,8; bonus bSPrecovRate,30;", "bonus bHPrecovRate,20; bonus bSPrecovRate,20;";
	setarray .@fx$, "", "+15 ATK, +10 HIT", "+8% max HP, faster HP recovery", "+8% max SP, faster SP recovery", "faster HP and SP recovery";
	// The client's food icon closest to the meal (each icon has the game's own fixed
	// tooltip, so the gold line above says what the meal really does).
	setarray .@ic, 0, EFST_FOOD_STR, EFST_FOOD_VIT, EFST_FOOD_INT, EFST_FOOD_VIT;
	// Bonds (3.7.0): a Trusted (or closer) cook's meal lasts 15 minutes longer.
	.@mdur = 1800;
	if (.mem) {
		if (callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, @cl_cid[.@k])) >= 3)
			.@mdur = 2700;
	}
	bonus_script .@bs$[.@meal], .@mdur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, .@ic[.@meal];
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] > 0)
			bonus_script .@bs$[.@meal], .@mdur, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, .@ic[.@meal], @cl_cid[.@i];
	}
	.@t$ = replacestr(callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, "cook" + .@meal, 0), .@k, ""), "{i}", .@mn$[.@meal]);
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@k];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@k];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = ET_DELIGHT;
	dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@k] + " cooked " + .@mn$[.@meal] + " (" + .@used$ + "): " + .@fx$[.@meal] + " for everyone, " + (.@mdur / 60) + " minutes.", 0xFFCC66;
	return;

// "<account id>:<item id>" of the first bag (companions', then yours) holding one of
// getarg(0) ("id,id,..."), or "".
L_CampFind:
	explode(.@ids$, getarg(0), ",");
	.@n = getarraysize(.@ids$);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
			if (countitem(atoi(.@ids$[.@j]), @cl_aid[.@i]) > 0)
				return @cl_aid[.@i] + ":" + .@ids$[.@j];
		}
	}
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
		if (countitem(atoi(.@ids$[.@j])) > 0)
			return getcharid(3) + ":" + .@ids$[.@j];
	}
	return "";

// Takes the one L_CampFind found; returns its name.
L_CampUse:
	explode(.@p$, getarg(0), ":");
	.@id = atoi(.@p$[1]);
	if (atoi(.@p$[0]) == getcharid(3))
		delitem .@id, 1;
	else
		delitem .@id, 1, atoi(.@p$[0]);
	return getitemname(.@id);

// The Companions status window: "@clstat <request> list". One record per
// companion in the party (on this map or not), fields split by |, records by ~:
// name|job|base lv|job lv|hp|max hp|sp|max sp|state|map|days|kills|mvps|fell|
// places|met map|your level then|first mvp|your falls they saw|closest to
// ("you", a companion's name, or "" when it isn't here)
// state: ok, rest, dead, away (another map), off (not here at all).
OnCmdStat:
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@out$ = "";
	.@pid = getcharid(1);
	if (.@pid) {
		.@me = getcharid(3);
		.@mymap$ = strcharinfo(3);
		getpartymember .@pid, 1;
		.@cnt = $@partymembercount;
		copyarray .@cid[0], $@partymembercid[0], .@cnt;
		getpartymember .@pid, 2;
		copyarray .@aid[0], $@partymemberaid[0], .@cnt;
		getpartymember .@pid, 0;
		copyarray .@nm$[0], $@partymembername$[0], .@cnt;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
			if (.@cid[.@i] < 95000000 || .@aid[.@i] == .@me)
				continue;
			.@c = .@cid[.@i];
			.@hp = readparam(Hp, .@c);
			.@r$ = callsub(L_Clean, .@nm$[.@i]);
			if (.@hp < 0) {
				.@r$ += "||0|0|0|0|0|0|off|";
			} else {
				.@map$ = strcharinfo(3, .@c);
				.@st$ = "ok";
				if (.@hp == 0)
					.@st$ = "dead";
				else if (.@map$ != .@mymap$)
					.@st$ = "away";
				else if (getd("@cl_rst_" + .@aid[.@i]))
					.@st$ = "rest";
				.@r$ += "|" + jobname(readparam(Class, .@c)) + "|" + readparam(BaseLevel, .@c) + "|" + readparam(JobLevel, .@c)
					+ "|" + .@hp + "|" + readparam(MaxHp, .@c) + "|" + readparam(Sp, .@c) + "|" + readparam(MaxSp, .@c)
					+ "|" + .@st$ + "|" + .@map$;
			}
			.@p$ = "m.c" + .@c;
			.@met = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", 0);
			.@days = 0;
			if (.@met > 0)
				.@days = (gettimetick(2) - .@met) / 86400;
			.@metmap$ = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".metmap", "");
			.@fmvp$ = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", "");
			.@r$ += "|" + .@days + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 0) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 0)
				+ "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".fell", 0) + "|" + modstore_count("char", .@p$ + ".maps")
				+ "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@metmap$) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".metlv", 0)
				+ "|" + callsub(L_Clean, .@fmvp$) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".sawfall", 0);
			// Who it's closest to (L_Fav), when it's here with you.
			.@ix = inarray(@cl_cid, .@c);
			.@fav$ = "";
			if (.@ix >= 0) {	// (two ifs: && reads both sides)
				if (@cl_hp[.@ix] > 0) {
					.@fx = callsub(L_Fav, .@ix);
					.@fav$ = "you";
					if (.@fx >= 0)
						.@fav$ = callsub(L_Clean, @cl_nm$[.@fx]);
				}
			}
			.@r$ += "|" + .@fav$;
			// 3.7.0, Bonds: |points|tier|camps|gifts
			.@pt = callsub(L_BondPts, .@c);
			.@r$ += "|" + .@pt + "|" + callsub(L_BondTier, .@pt) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".camps", 0) + "|" + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".gifts", 0);
			if (.@out$ != "")
				.@out$ += "~";
			.@out$ += .@r$;
		}
	}
	.@len = getstrlen(.@out$);
	.@n = max(1, (.@len + 179) / 180);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@part$ = "";
		if (.@len > 0)
			.@part$ = substr(.@out$, .@k * 180, min(.@len, (.@k + 1) * 180) - 1);
		dispbottom "@@reply " + .@req$ + " " + (.@k + 1) + "/" + .@n + " " + .@part$;
	}
	end;

// The Companions window's Bag tab: "@clbag <request> <action>".
//   list                      P|purse|inventory on|zeny per level, then per
//                             companion C|char id|name|job|outfit colour|min|
//                             max|hair colour|min|max, and per stack in
//                             its bag I|char id|index|item id|amount|worn|
//                             refine|cards|options|bound|rental|name (cards
//                             "id,id", options "id:value,id:value")
//                             (records split by ~); worn is
//                             the equip slots (0 = in the bag), and only gear
//                             you gave is listed, never its own
//   take <char id> <index> <amount>   that stack (or part of it) to you
//   gearback <char id> <equip slots>  worn gear you gave, back to you
//   dye <char id> cloth|hair <colour> outfit or hair colour, kept for good
//   withdraw <zeny> / deposit <zeny>  the party purse
// 3.1.0: the C record ends with |weight|max weight (tenths, as the game counts),
//   and P with |in town (1/0); per restock entry R|char id|item id|keep|name.
//   rsset <char id> <item id> <keep>  a companion's restock list (0 = off the list);
//                                     in town it shops at once
//   lootlist                  P|purse|trip start|Overcharge %|finds on (1/0), then
//                             per item L|item id|amount|found at|sell price|name
//   ltake <item id> <amount>  out of the party bag, to you
//   lsell <item id> <amount>  sold, the zeny into the purse
// Actions answer OK|text or ERR|text. A companion's bag is its own (the
// engine's companion inventory); the purse is the party's (mod store, char
// scope, "purse"). The bag is read with getinventorylist <char id>, which
// leaves its @inventorylist_* on the companion; getvar reads them from there.
OnCmdBag:
	// A full party's bags (arrows, potions, gear: 30+ stacks, each read field by
	// field) is more than the loop guard allows; it stopped the list half way
	// ("infinity loop"), so the window kept showing an old list. It ends with
	// this run.
	freeloop(1);
	.@req$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[0];
	.@act$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[1];
	.@inv = getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_inventory");
	if (.@act$ == "list") {
		.@out$ = "P|" + modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) + "|" + .@inv + "|" + .purse + "|" + (callsub(L_MapKind, strcharinfo(3)) == "town");
		callsub L_BagParty;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @clb_n; .@i++) {
			.@c = @clb_cid[.@i];
			.@cls = readparam(Class, .@c);
			.@fx = 0;
			if (checkriding(.@c))
				.@fx += 32;
			if (checkfalcon(.@c))
				.@fx += 16;
			.@out$ += "~C|" + .@c + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, @clb_nm$[.@i]) + "|" + jobname(.@cls)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@c) + "|" + getbattleflag("min_cloth_color") + "|" + callsub(L_ClothMax, .@cls)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@c) + "|" + getbattleflag("min_hair_color") + "|" + getbattleflag("max_hair_color")
				+ "|" + readparam(Weight, .@c) + "|" + readparam(MaxWeight, .@c)
				// 3.5.0: the look, for the Bag tab's character (the client's api.screens.stage):
				// class, sex, hair style, then the view ids the game itself sends: weapon,
				// shield, lower/top/middle headgear, garment; and the mount/falcon bits
				// (OPTION_RIDING 0x20, OPTION_FALCON 0x10).
				+ "|" + .@cls + "|" + readparam(Sex, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_HAIR, .@c)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_WEAPON, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_SHIELD, .@c)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_HEAD_BOTTOM, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_HEAD_TOP, .@c) + "|" + getlook(LOOK_HEAD_MID, .@c)
				+ "|" + getlook(LOOK_ROBE, .@c) + "|" + .@fx;
			// Its restock list.
			.@rn = modstore_keys("char", "rs.c" + .@c, .@rids$);
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@rn; .@j++) {
				.@rid = atoi(.@rids$[.@j]);
				.@out$ += "~R|" + .@c + "|" + .@rid + "|" + modstore_get("char", "rs.c" + .@c + "." + .@rid, 0) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, getitemname(.@rid));
			}
			// The slots worn with gear you gave (the engine's given_mask). 3.5.0: its own
			// starting gear is listed too, marked own (the Bag tab draws every slot like the
			// Equip window); it can't be taken.
			.@gm = 0;
			if (query_sql("SELECT given_mask FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0)
					+ " AND name = '" + escape_sql(@clb_nm$[.@i]) + "' LIMIT 1", .@gmv) > 0)
				.@gm = .@gmv;
			// Gear you dropped on it in the Bag tab (our own record), and the pieces
			// of its own that came off for it (in its bag, not yours to take).
			.@gm = .@gm | callsub(L_DgMask, .@c);
			.@own$ = callsub(L_DgOwn, .@c);
			getinventorylist .@c;
			.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
			for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
				.@eq = getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c);
				.@mine = 0;
				if (.@eq && .@eq != EQP_AMMO && !(.@eq & .@gm))
					.@mine = 1;	// its own gear
				.@id = getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c);
				if (!.@eq && compare(.@own$, "," + .@id + ","))
					continue;	// its own piece, waiting to go back on
				// Cards in its slots (card ids, not a forged or brewed item's maker),
				// as "id,id"; the window shows their names.
				.@cards$ = "";
				.@c1 = getvar(@inventorylist_card1[.@j], .@c);
				if (.@c1 != 255 && .@c1 != 254) {
					setarray .@cv, .@c1, getvar(@inventorylist_card2[.@j], .@c),
						getvar(@inventorylist_card3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_card4[.@j], .@c);
					for (.@s = 0; .@s < 4; .@s++) {
						if (.@cv[.@s] > 1000 && .@cv[.@s] < 100000000)
							.@cards$ += (.@cards$ == "" ? "" : ",") + .@cv[.@s];
					}
				}
				// Random options (Affix Forge and the like) as "id:value,id:value";
				// the window turns them into "STR +3".
				setarray .@ov, getvar(@inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value3[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], .@c),
					getvar(@inventorylist_option_id5[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value5[.@j], .@c);
				.@opts$ = "";
				for (.@s = 0; .@s < 10; .@s += 2) {
					if (.@ov[.@s] > 0)
						.@opts$ += (.@opts$ == "" ? "" : ",") + .@ov[.@s] + ":" + .@ov[.@s + 1];
				}
				.@out$ += "~I|" + .@c + "|" + getvar(@inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@c) + "|" + .@id
					+ "|" + getvar(@inventorylist_amount[.@j], .@c) + "|" + .@eq
					+ "|" + getvar(@inventorylist_refine[.@j], .@c) + "|" + .@cards$ + "|" + .@opts$
					+ "|" + (getvar(@inventorylist_bound[.@j], .@c) > 0) + "|" + (getvar(@inventorylist_expire[.@j], .@c) > 0)
					+ "|" + .@mine + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, getitemname(.@id));
			}
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@out$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "take") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@idx = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		.@amt = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]);
		if (!.@inv) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Turn on Settings > Population > Companion inventory first. Without it a companion's potions and arrows are free and refill by themselves, so there's nothing of yours in its bag.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		getinventorylist .@c;
		.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
			if (getvar(@inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@c) == .@idx)
				break;
		}
		if (.@j >= .@cnt) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That item isn't in the bag any more.";
			end;
		}
		.@id = getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c);
		.@have = getvar(@inventorylist_amount[.@j], .@c);
		if (getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c) != 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + @clb_nm$[.@k] + " is wearing that. Worn gear comes back through the app's Companions window (Gear).";
			end;
		}
		// Rentals stay: a copy would come back without its time limit.
		if (getvar(@inventorylist_expire[.@j], .@c)) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That one is a rental, it can't be handed over.";
			end;
		}
		if (compare(callsub(L_DgOwn, .@c), "," + .@id + ",")) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That's " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + "'s own, it goes back on when you take back what you gave.";
			end;
		}
		.@amt = min(max(.@amt, 1), .@have);
		if (!checkweight(.@id, .@amt)) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|You can't carry that much.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_ToYou, .@c, .@j, .@amt;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Took " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " from " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + ".";
		end;
	}
	// Test build (2.3.0): "give <char id> <index> <item id> <amount>", an item
	// dragged from your inventory onto a companion in the Bag tab. It goes into
	// its bag, with refine, cards, random options and bound type. Gear it can
	// wear it puts on at once; the engine's given_mask is only set by a real
	// trade, so that slot is "from you" in our own record (mod store, char
	// scope, dg.c<char id>: "<slots>,<item id>,<its own item id>" entries split
	// by ";"), which gearback below reads.
	if (.@act$ == "give") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@idx = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		.@want = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]);
		.@amt = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[5]);
		if (!.@inv) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Turn on Settings > Population > Companion inventory first; without it a companion has no bag of its own.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		.@aid = 0;
		if (.@k >= 0)
			.@aid = getcharid(3, @clb_nm$[.@k]);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0 || .@aid <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't here.";
			end;
		}
		.@nm$ = @clb_nm$[.@k];
		// Your stack: the index the window saw, or else the first loose stack of it.
		getinventorylist;
		.@j = -1;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @inventorylist_count; .@i++) {
			if (@inventorylist_id[.@i] != .@want || @inventorylist_equip[.@i])
				continue;
			if (@inventorylist_idx[.@i] == .@idx) {
				.@j = .@i;
				break;
			}
			if (.@j < 0)
				.@j = .@i;
		}
		if (.@j < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That isn't in your inventory (take it off first if you wear it).";
			end;
		}
		if (@inventorylist_expire[.@j]) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Rentals can't be handed over.";
			end;
		}
		.@id = .@want;
		.@idx = @inventorylist_idx[.@j];
		// (Copied out first: min() given an array element reads the whole rest of
		// the array, so it came out as the smallest stack after this one.)
		.@have = @inventorylist_amount[.@j];
		.@amt = min(max(.@amt, 1), .@have);
		.@iw = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_WEIGHT);
		.@room = readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) - readparam(Weight, .@c);
		if (.@iw * .@amt > .@room) {
			// How many would fit, and the weight in the game's units (tenths).
			.@fit = 0;
			if (.@iw > 0 && .@room > 0)
				.@fit = .@room / .@iw;
			.@wt$ = " (weight " + (readparam(Weight, .@c) / 10) + " / " + (readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) / 10) + ")";
			if (.@fit > 0)
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + .@nm$ + " can only carry " + .@fit + " more " + getitemname(.@id) + .@wt$ + ". Take something heavy back first, or give fewer.";
			else
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + .@nm$ + "'s bag is full" + .@wt$ + ". Take something heavy back first.";
			end;
		}
		.@ident = @inventorylist_identify[.@j];
		.@ref = @inventorylist_refine[.@j];
		.@attr = @inventorylist_attribute[.@j];
		.@c1 = @inventorylist_card1[.@j];
		.@c2 = @inventorylist_card2[.@j];
		.@c3 = @inventorylist_card3[.@j];
		.@c4 = @inventorylist_card4[.@j];
		.@bound = @inventorylist_bound[.@j];
		setarray .@oid, @inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id3[.@j],
			@inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_id5[.@j];
		setarray .@oval, @inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value3[.@j],
			@inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_value5[.@j];
		setarray .@opar, @inventorylist_option_parameter1[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter2[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter3[.@j],
			@inventorylist_option_parameter4[.@j], @inventorylist_option_parameter5[.@j];
		// Into its bag first, then out of yours.
		if (.@bound)
			getitembound3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@bound, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar, .@aid;
		else
			getitem3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar, .@aid;
		delitemidx .@idx, .@amt;
		.@msg$ = "Gave " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " to " + .@nm$ + ".";
		// Gear: it puts it on, as a trade does. Whatever you gave for that slot
		// before comes back to you first (a trade swaps it the same way).
		.@loc = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_LOCATIONS);
		.@type = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_TYPE);
		if ((.@type == IT_WEAPON || .@type == IT_ARMOR) && .@loc > 0 && .@amt == 1 && .@ident) {
			.@gm = 0;
			if (query_sql("SELECT given_mask FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0)
					+ " AND name = '" + escape_sql(.@nm$) + "' LIMIT 1", .@gmv) > 0)
				.@gm = .@gmv;
			.@dm = callsub(L_DgMask, .@c);
			// The slot. An accessory with a side free just goes on (rAthena fills
			// the right when it's empty, else the left) and nothing comes off. With
			// both sides worn it replaces the companion's own piece, else the left
			// one. (EQP_ACC_RL: a plain EQP_ACC is no constant here, just an empty
			// variable, so every ring was taken for "both sides" and sent the worn
			// one back, 2.4.0.)
			.@t = .@loc;
			if (.@loc == EQP_ACC_RL) {
				if (getequipid(EQI_ACC_L, .@c) <= 0 || getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) <= 0)
					.@t = 0;
				else if (((.@gm | .@dm) & EQP_ACC_L) && !((.@gm | .@dm) & EQP_ACC_R))
					.@t = EQP_ACC_R;
				else
					.@t = EQP_ACC_L;
			}
			.@ok = 1;
			.@extra$ = "";
			if (.@t & .@dm) {
				.@back$ = callsub(L_DgBack, .@c, .@nm$, .@t & .@dm);
				if (substr(.@back$, 0, 2) == "ER") {
					.@ok = 0;
					.@msg$ += " It's in the bag: " + substr(.@back$, 4, getstrlen(.@back$) - 1);
				} else if (.@back$ != "") {
					.@extra$ += " " + substr(.@back$, 3, getstrlen(.@back$) - 1);
				}
			}
			if (.@ok && (.@t & .@gm)) {
				// Traded gear: the engine's own way back (as the Take back button).
				setarray .@bit, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_SHOES, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID;
				setarray .@word$, "headlow", "weapon", "garment", "accl", "armor", "shield", "shoes", "accr", "headtop", "headmid";
				.@slots$ = "";
				for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@bit); .@s++) {
					if (.@t & .@gm & .@bit[.@s])
						.@slots$ += " " + .@word$[.@s];
				}
				if (.@t & .@gm & (EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW | EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT))
					.@slots$ += " costume";
				if (.@slots$ != "") {
					atcommand "@companion gear " + .@nm$ + .@slots$;
					.@extra$ += " What you gave for that slot went back to you.";
				}
			}
			if (.@ok) {
				setarray .@eb, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_SHOES,
					EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT;
				setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES,
					EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQI_COSTUME_GARMENT;
				for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++)
					.@old[.@s] = (.@t & .@eb[.@s]) ? getequipid(.@ei[.@s], .@c) : 0;
				// rAthena puts an accessory on the right unless only the left is free,
				// so the chosen side is emptied first (its piece is in .@old).
				if (.@t == EQP_ACC_R && getequipid(EQI_ACC_R, .@c) > 0)
					unequip EQI_ACC_R, .@c;
				else if (.@t == EQP_ACC_L && getequipid(EQI_ACC_L, .@c) > 0)
					unequip EQI_ACC_L, .@c;
				// (This server has no equip2: by item id, so the first of it in its bag.)
				equip .@id, .@c;
				// Where it went (an accessory picks a side, a two-hander takes both hands).
				.@m = 0;
				getinventorylist .@c;
				.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
				for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
					if (getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@i], .@c) == .@id && (getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@i], .@c) & .@loc)) {
						.@m = getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@i], .@c);
						break;
					}
				}
				if (.@m) {
					.@own = 0;
					for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++) {
						if ((.@m & .@eb[.@s]) && .@old[.@s] > 0) {
							.@own = .@old[.@s];
							break;
						}
					}
					.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
					modstore_set "char", "dg.c" + .@c, .@rec$ + (.@rec$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@m + "," + .@id + "," + .@own;
					.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " put on the " + getitemname(.@id) + "." + .@extra$;
				} else {
					.@msg$ += " " + .@nm$ + " can't wear it, so it's in the bag." + .@extra$;
				}
			}
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	// Dye: "dye <char id> cloth|hair <colour>". Shown at once (setlook), and kept
	// in the mod store (char scope, dye.c<char id>.cl / .hc), because the engine
	// saves a companion's colours only when it is recruited; L_Dyes puts them
	// back on whenever the engine respawns it with its old ones.
	if (.@act$ == "dye") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@kind$ = .@atcmd_parameters$[3];
		.@v = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0 || readparam(Hp, .@c) < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't here.";
			end;
		}
		if (.@kind$ == "cloth") {
			.@v = cap_value(.@v, getbattleflag("min_cloth_color"), callsub(L_ClothMax, readparam(Class, .@c)));
			setlook LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
			modstore_set "char", "dye.c" + .@c + ".cl", .@v;
		} else if (.@kind$ == "hair") {
			.@v = cap_value(.@v, getbattleflag("min_hair_color"), getbattleflag("max_hair_color"));
			setlook LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
			modstore_set "char", "dye.c" + .@c + ".hc", .@v;
		} else {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Unknown dye.";
			end;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|";
		end;
	}
	// Worn gear you gave: the engine's own "@companion gear <name> <slots>" (the
	// app's Gear tab sends the same), so it goes back to you its usual way and
	// the companion puts its own piece back on. Costume slots go back together.
	if (.@act$ == "gearback") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@eq = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0 || .@eq <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		// Gear you dropped on it in the Bag tab (test build 2.3.0): our own record,
		// not the engine's. Off, back to you, and its own piece back on.
		.@back$ = callsub(L_DgBack, .@c, @clb_nm$[.@k], .@eq);
		if (.@back$ != "") {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@back$;
			end;
		}
		setarray .@bit, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_SHOES, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID;
		setarray .@word$, "headlow", "weapon", "garment", "accl", "armor", "shield", "shoes", "accr", "headtop", "headmid";
		.@slots$ = "";
