		for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@bit); .@s++) {
			if (.@eq & .@bit[.@s])
				.@slots$ += " " + .@word$[.@s];
		}
		if (.@eq & (EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID | EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW | EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT))
			.@slots$ += " costume";
		if (.@eq & (EQP_SHADOW_ARMOR | EQP_SHADOW_WEAPON | EQP_SHADOW_SHIELD | EQP_SHADOW_SHOES | EQP_SHADOW_ACC_R | EQP_SHADOW_ACC_L))
			.@slots$ += " shadow";
		if (.@slots$ == "") {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Not a slot that can be handed back.";
			end;
		}
		atcommand "@companion gear " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + .@slots$;
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Asked " + @clb_nm$[.@k] + " to hand it back (see your chat).";
		end;
	}
	// 3.1.0: a companion's restock list. An item dragged onto its Restock row
	// (or a changed amount): keep this many; 0 takes it off the list.
	if (.@act$ == "rsset") {
		.@c = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@id = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]);
		.@keep = cap_value(atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[4]), 0, 30000);
		callsub L_BagParty;
		.@k = inarray(@clb_cid, .@c);
		if (.@c < 95000000 || .@k < 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That companion isn't in your party.";
			end;
		}
		.@nm$ = @clb_nm$[.@k];
		.@key$ = "rs.c" + .@c + "." + .@id;
		if (!.@keep) {
			modstore_delete "char", .@key$;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + getitemname(.@id) + " is off " + .@nm$ + "'s restock list.";
			end;
		}
		if (!compare(.rs_ok$, "," + .@id + ",")) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + getitemname(.@id) + " isn't sold in NPC shops, so it can't be restocked. Potions, gemstones, arrows, bullets and the like can.";
			end;
		}
		if (!modstore_exists("char", .@key$) && modstore_count("char", "rs.c" + .@c) >= .rs_max) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|" + .@nm$ + "'s list is full (" + .rs_max + " items). Take one off first.";
			end;
		}
		modstore_set "char", .@key$, .@keep;
		.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " keeps " + .@keep + " " + getitemname(.@id) + ", restocked in town.";
		// 3.1.1: dropped from your inventory (index getarg 5): your stack goes in first,
		// up to the number to keep; the purse only buys what's still missing. (3.1.0
		// only bought from the purse, so your stack stayed and it looked doubled.)
		if (.@atcmd_numparameters > 5 && .@inv) {
			.@from = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[5]);
			.@aid = getcharid(3, .@nm$);
			.@need = 0;
			if (.@aid > 0)
				.@need = .@keep - countitem(.@id, .@aid);
			if (.@need > 0) {
				getinventorylist;
				.@j = -1;
				for (.@i = 0; .@i < @inventorylist_count; .@i++) {
					if (@inventorylist_id[.@i] != .@id || @inventorylist_equip[.@i] || @inventorylist_expire[.@i] || @inventorylist_bound[.@i])
						continue;
					if (@inventorylist_idx[.@i] == .@from) {
						.@j = .@i;
						break;
					}
					if (.@j < 0)
						.@j = .@i;
				}
				if (.@j >= 0) {
					.@have = @inventorylist_amount[.@j];
					.@n = min(.@need, .@have);
					.@iw = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_WEIGHT);
					if (.@iw > 0)
						.@n = min(.@n, (readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) - readparam(Weight, .@c)) / .@iw);
					if (.@n > 0) {
						getitem .@id, .@n, .@aid;
						delitemidx @inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@n;
						.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " keeps " + .@keep + " " + getitemname(.@id) + ": " + .@n + " from you now, the rest restocked in town.";
						if (.@n >= .@need)
							.@msg$ = .@nm$ + " keeps " + .@keep + " " + getitemname(.@id) + ": " + .@n + " from you now, topped up in town from here on.";
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (callsub(L_MapKind, strcharinfo(3)) == "town" && .@inv) {
			callsub L_Restock, .@c;
			if (@rs_msg$ != "")
				.@msg$ += " " + @rs_msg$;
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	// 3.1.0: the Loot tab. The party bag lives in the mod store (char scope:
	// loot.<item id> = how many, lootat.<item id> = when it was last found).
	if (.@act$ == "lootlist") {
		.@oc = callsub(L_Discount, 38);
		.@out$ = "P|" + modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) + "|" + modstore_get("char", "lttrip", 0) + "|" + .@oc + "|" + (.loot_share > 0);
		.@n = modstore_keys("char", "loot", .@ids$);
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
			.@id = atoi(.@ids$[.@j]);
			.@amt = modstore_get("char", "loot." + .@id, 0);
			if (.@amt <= 0)
				continue;
			.@out$ += "~L|" + .@id + "|" + .@amt + "|" + modstore_get("char", "lootat." + .@id, 0)
				+ "|" + (getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_SELL) * (100 + .@oc) / 100) + "|" + callsub(L_Clean, getitemname(.@id));
		}
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, .@out$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "ltake" || .@act$ == "lsell") {
		.@id = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@have = modstore_get("char", "loot." + .@id, 0);
		.@amt = min(max(atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[3]), 1), .@have);
		if (.@have <= 0) {
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|That isn't in the party bag any more.";
			end;
		}
		if (.@act$ == "ltake") {
			if (!checkweight(.@id, .@amt)) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|You can't carry that much.";
				end;
			}
			getitem .@id, .@amt;
			.@msg$ = "Took " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " from the party bag.";
		} else {
			.@oc = callsub(L_Discount, 38);
			.@z = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_SELL) * (100 + .@oc) / 100 * .@amt;
			if (modstore_get("char", "purse", 0) + .@z > 1000000000) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|The purse is full (1,000,000,000 z). Take some zeny out first.";
				end;
			}
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", .@z);
			.@msg$ = "Sold " + .@amt + " x " + getitemname(.@id) + " for " + callfunc("F_InsertComma", .@z) + " z, into the purse.";
			if (.@oc > 0)
				.@msg$ += " (" + @rs_who$ + "'s Overcharge +" + .@oc + "%)";
		}
		if (.@have - .@amt <= 0) {
			modstore_delete "char", "loot." + .@id;
			modstore_delete "char", "lootat." + .@id;
		} else
			modstore_inc("char", "loot." + .@id, -.@amt);
		callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|" + .@msg$;
		end;
	}
	if (.@act$ == "withdraw" || .@act$ == "deposit") {
		.@z = atoi(.@atcmd_parameters$[2]);
		.@p = modstore_get("char", "purse", 0);
		if (.@act$ == "withdraw") {
			.@z = min(.@z, .@p, 1000000000 - Zeny);
			if (.@z <= 0) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Nothing to take out.";
				end;
			}
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", -.@z);
			Zeny += .@z;
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Took " + .@z + " zeny out of the purse.";
		} else {
			.@z = min(.@z, Zeny, 1000000000 - .@p);
			if (.@z <= 0) {
				callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Nothing to put in.";
				end;
			}
			Zeny -= .@z;
			modstore_inc("char", "purse", .@z);
			callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "OK|Put " + .@z + " zeny in the purse.";
		}
		end;
	}
	callsub L_BagReply, .@req$, "ERR|Unknown request.";
	end;

// The companions in your party that are loaded (on any map): @clb_n of them,
// char ids in @clb_cid, names in @clb_nm$ (character variables: a callsub
// has .@ variables of its own).
L_BagParty:
	@clb_n = 0;
	deletearray @clb_cid;
	deletearray @clb_nm$;
	.@pid = getcharid(1);
	if (!.@pid)
		return;
	getpartymember .@pid, 1;
	.@pc = $@partymembercount;
	copyarray .@pcid[0], $@partymembercid[0], .@pc;
	getpartymember .@pid, 0;
	copyarray .@pnm$[0], $@partymembername$[0], .@pc;
	for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@pc; .@q++) {
		if (.@pcid[.@q] < 95000000 || readparam(Hp, .@pcid[.@q]) < 0)
			continue;
		@clb_cid[@clb_n] = .@pcid[.@q];
		@clb_nm$[@clb_n] = .@pnm$[.@q];
		@clb_n++;
	}
	return;

// 3.1.0, the loot bag: party kills. Scripts get no event for a companion's kill
// (the engine drops script events for its shells), so every tick this looks at the
// monsters within 9 cells of you or a companion. One seen last tick that is now
// gone, at 0 HP, or back at full HP far away (a map spawn keeps its id and comes
// back elsewhere) was beaten: a party kill, whoever landed it. Slaves don't count.
// Last tick's monsters: @lt_g (id), @lt_c (class), @lt_x/@lt_y, @lt_n of them.
L_LootTrack:
	getmapxy(.@m$, .@x, .@y, BL_PC);
	if (.@m$ != @lt_map$) {
		@lt_map$ = .@m$;
		@lt_n = 0;
	}
	if (getmapflag(.@m$, MF_NOMOBLOOT)) {
		@lt_n = 0;
		return;
	}
	// Where the party stands (you first), and the box round all of you.
	.@px[0] = .@x;
	.@py[0] = .@y;
	.@np = 1;
	.@x1 = .@x - 10;
	.@x2 = .@x + 10;
	.@y1 = .@y - 10;
	.@y2 = .@y + 10;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@m$)
			continue;
		.@ddx = .@cx - .@x;
		.@ddy = .@cy - .@y;
		if (max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy) > 40)
			continue;	// far off on its own
		.@px[.@np] = .@cx;
		.@py[.@np] = .@cy;
		.@np++;
		.@x1 = min(.@x1, .@cx - 10);
		.@x2 = max(.@x2, .@cx + 10);
		.@y1 = min(.@y1, .@cy - 10);
		.@y2 = max(.@y2, .@cy + 10);
	}
	// Last tick's monsters: which were beaten?
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < @lt_n; .@k++) {
		.@g = @lt_g[.@k];
		.@dead = 0;
		if (!unitexists(.@g))
			.@dead = 1;
		else {
			getunitdata .@g, .@d;
			.@hp = .@d[UMOB_HP];
			.@ddx = .@d[UMOB_X] - @lt_x[.@k];
			.@ddy = .@d[UMOB_Y] - @lt_y[.@k];
			if (.@hp <= 0)
				.@dead = 1;
			else if (.@hp >= .@d[UMOB_MAXHP] && max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy) > 15)
				.@dead = 1;	// beaten, and already back somewhere else
		}
		if (.@dead)
			callsub L_LootKill, @lt_c[.@k], @lt_x[.@k], @lt_y[.@k];
	}
	// This tick's monsters near one of you.
	@lt_n = 0;
	.@n = getareaunits(BL_MOB, .@m$, .@x1, .@y1, .@x2, .@y2, .@u);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n && @lt_n < 64; .@k++) {
		.@g = .@u[.@k];
		getunitdata .@g, .@d;
		if (.@d[UMOB_HP] <= 0 || .@d[UMOB_MASTERAID] > 0)
			continue;
		.@mx = .@d[UMOB_X];
		.@my = .@d[UMOB_Y];
		for (.@p = 0; .@p < .@np; .@p++) {
			.@ddx = .@mx - .@px[.@p];
			.@ddy = .@my - .@py[.@p];
			if (max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy) <= 9)
				break;
		}
		if (.@p >= .@np)
			continue;
		@lt_g[@lt_n] = .@g;
		@lt_c[@lt_n] = .@d[UMOB_CLASS];
		@lt_x[@lt_n] = .@mx;
		@lt_y[@lt_n] = .@my;
		@lt_n++;
	}
	return;

// A party kill of monster class getarg(0) at getarg(1),getarg(2): maybe one thing
// from its drop list into the party bag (mod store, char scope: loot.<item id> =
// how many, lootat.<item id> = when it was last found). Each drop is tried at
// .loot_share per thousand of its normal rate, from a random place in the list.
// Cards, gear and drops of 1% or rarer are rare finds: a line, and the name of
// the companion standing closest.
L_LootKill:
	.@cls = getarg(0);
	if (!getmobdrops(.@cls) || $@MobDrop_count < 1)
		return;
	.@c = $@MobDrop_count;
	copyarray .@it[0], $@MobDrop_item[0], .@c;
	copyarray .@rt[0], $@MobDrop_rate[0], .@c;
	.@s = 0;
	if (.@c > 1)
		.@s = rand(.@c);
	.@id = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@c; .@k++) {
		.@j = (.@s + .@k) % .@c;
		if (.@it[.@j] <= 0 || .@rt[.@j] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (rand(10000000) < .@rt[.@j] * .loot_share) {
			.@id = .@it[.@j];
			.@r = .@rt[.@j];
			break;
		}
	}
	if (!.@id)
		return;
	modstore_inc("char", "loot." + .@id, 1);
	modstore_set "char", "lootat." + .@id, gettimetick(2);
	@lt_found++;
	.@type = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_TYPE);
	if (.@type != IT_CARD && .@type != IT_WEAPON && .@type != IT_ARMOR && .@r > 100)
		return;	// a common find: it's in the Loot tab, nothing said
	// The best of the trip, for the summary in town: a card over anything else.
	if (@lt_best$ == "" || .@type == IT_CARD)
		@lt_best$ = getitemname(.@id);
	// Who found it: the companion standing closest to where it fell.
	.@b = -1;
	.@bd = 99;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@i]) != 0)
			continue;
		.@ddx = .@cx - getarg(1);
		.@ddy = .@cy - getarg(2);
		.@dd = max(.@ddx, -.@ddx, .@ddy, -.@ddy);
		if (.@dd < .@bd) {
			.@bd = .@dd;
			.@b = .@i;
		}
	}
	if (.@b < 0) {
		dispbottom "[Loot] Found: " + getitemname(.@id) + ". It's in the party bag (Companions window, Loot tab).", 0xFFCC66;
		return;
	}
	dispbottom "[Loot] " + @cl_nm$[.@b] + " found " + getitemname(.@id) + "! It's in the party bag (Loot tab).", 0xFFCC66;
	// A word about it, at most every 15 s (a card always).
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	if (.chatter && (.@type == IT_CARD || .@sec >= @lt_saycd)) {
		@lt_saycd = .@sec + 15;
		.@em = ET_DELIGHT;
		if (.@type == IT_CARD)
			.@em = ET_THROB;
		callsub L_QueueFmt, .@b, "found", "", .@em, 0, getitemname(.@id);
	}
	return;

// Back in town: what the trip turned up (@lt_found finds since the last town,
// @lt_best$ the best), told once.
L_LootHaul:
	if (@lt_found <= 0)
		return;
	.@what$ = @lt_found + " thing" + (@lt_found == 1 ? "" : "s");
	if (@lt_best$ != "")
		.@what$ += ", the best a " + @lt_best$;
	dispbottom "[Loot] This trip: " + .@what$ + ". See the Loot tab in the Companions window (Alt+N).", 0xFFCC66;
	if (.chatter && @lt_best$ != "") {
		.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "haul", "", ET_MONEY, @lt_found, @lt_best$;
	}
	@lt_found = 0;
	@lt_best$ = "";
	return;

// Restock in town: each companion here gets its list (mod store, char scope,
// rs.c<char id>.<item id> = how many to keep) topped up, bought at the NPC shop
// price from the party purse, less the party's best Discount. getarg(0): one
// companion's char id, or 0 for all. Needs the app's Companion inventory (without
// it their potions and arrows are free anyway). Returns the zeny spent; the
// summary is in @rs_msg$ (and in your chat box).
L_Restock:
	@rs_msg$ = "";
	if (!getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_inventory"))
		return 0;
	.@only = getarg(0);
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	callsub L_BagParty;
	.@cn = @clb_n;
	copyarray .@cc[0], @clb_cid[0], .@cn;
	copyarray .@cnm$[0], @clb_nm$[0], .@cn;
	.@disc = callsub(L_Discount, 37);
	.@dwho$ = @rs_who$;
	.@purse = modstore_get("char", "purse", 0);
	.@spent = 0;
	.@got$ = "";
	.@short$ = "";
	.@heavy$ = "";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@cn; .@k++) {
		.@c = .@cc[.@k];
		if ((.@only && .@c != .@only) || strcharinfo(3, .@c) != .@map$)
			continue;
		.@aid = getcharid(3, .@cnm$[.@k]);
		if (.@aid <= 0)
			continue;
		.@n = modstore_keys("char", "rs.c" + .@c, .@ids$);
		.@mine$ = "";
		for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@n; .@j++) {
			.@id = atoi(.@ids$[.@j]);
			.@want = modstore_get("char", "rs.c" + .@c + "." + .@id, 0);
			.@need = .@want - countitem(.@id, .@aid);
			if (.@need <= 0)
				continue;
			.@price = max(1, getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_BUY) * (100 - .@disc) / 100);
			.@buy = min(.@need, .@purse / .@price);
			.@iw = getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_WEIGHT);
			.@room = .@buy;
			if (.@iw > 0)
				.@room = (readparam(MaxWeight, .@c) - readparam(Weight, .@c)) / .@iw;
			if (.@room < .@buy) {
				.@buy = max(0, .@room);
				if (!compare(.@heavy$ + ",", ", " + .@cnm$[.@k] + ","))
					.@heavy$ += ", " + .@cnm$[.@k];
			}
			if (.@buy > 0) {
				getitem .@id, .@buy, .@aid;
				.@purse -= .@buy * .@price;
				.@spent += .@buy * .@price;
				.@mine$ += (.@mine$ == "" ? "" : ", ") + .@buy + " " + getitemname(.@id);
			}
			if (.@buy < .@need && .@room >= .@need)
				.@short$ += ", " + (.@need - .@buy) + " " + getitemname(.@id);
		}
		if (.@mine$ != "")
			.@got$ += (.@got$ == "" ? "" : "; ") + .@cnm$[.@k] + ": " + .@mine$;
	}
	if (.@spent > 0)
		modstore_inc("char", "purse", -.@spent);
	if (.@got$ != "") {
		@rs_msg$ = "Restocked " + .@got$ + " for " + callfunc("F_InsertComma", .@spent) + " z";
		if (.@disc > 0)
			@rs_msg$ += " (" + .@dwho$ + "'s Discount -" + .@disc + "%)";
		@rs_msg$ += ".";
	}
	if (.@short$ != "")
		@rs_msg$ += (@rs_msg$ == "" ? "" : " ") + "Not enough in the purse for" + substr(.@short$, 1, getstrlen(.@short$) - 1) + ".";
	if (.@heavy$ != "")
		@rs_msg$ += (@rs_msg$ == "" ? "" : " ") + "Too heavy to carry it all:" + substr(.@heavy$, 1, getstrlen(.@heavy$) - 1) + ".";
	if (@rs_msg$ != "")
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + @rs_msg$, 0xFFCC66;
	// A word from the one who bargained, else anyone.
	if (.@got$ != "" && .chatter) {
		.@a = -1;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
			if (@cl_nm$[.@i] == .@dwho$)
				.@a = .@i;
		}
		if (.@a < 0)
			.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
		if (.@a >= 0)
			callsub L_QueueFmt, .@a, "restock", "", ET_OK, .@spent, "";
	}
	return .@spent;

// The party's best Discount (skill getarg(0) = 37) or Overcharge (38) in percent:
// yours, or that of a Merchant-line companion in your party (its own skill level,
// read by attaching to it for a moment). @rs_who$ = whose it is.
L_Discount:
	.@sk = getarg(0);
	.@me = getcharid(3);
	.@best = getskilllv(.@sk);
	@rs_who$ = "";
	if (.@best > 0)
		@rs_who$ = strcharinfo(0);
	callsub L_BagParty;
	.@cn = @clb_n;
	copyarray .@cc[0], @clb_cid[0], .@cn;
	copyarray .@cnm$[0], @clb_nm$[0], .@cn;
	.@who$ = @rs_who$;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@cn; .@k++) {
		if ((eaclass(readparam(Class, .@cc[.@k])) & EAJ_BASEMASK) != EAJ_MERCHANT)
			continue;
		.@aid = getcharid(3, .@cnm$[.@k]);
		if (.@aid <= 0 || !attachrid(.@aid))
			continue;
		.@lv = getskilllv(.@sk);
		attachrid(.@me);
		if (.@lv > .@best) {
			.@best = .@lv;
			.@who$ = .@cnm$[.@k];
		}
	}
	@rs_who$ = .@who$;
	if (.@best <= 0)
		return 0;
	if (.@best >= 10)
		return 24;
	return 5 + 2 * .@best;	// 7% at level 1, +2% a level, 24% at 10

// The names of your companions set to roam (@cl_rm$), from the engine's table;
// none while the app's Companion roaming setting is off (they follow then).
L_RoamRead:
	deletearray @cl_rm$;
	if (getbattleflag("population_engine_companion_roam") > 0)
		query_sql "SELECT name FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0) + " AND roam = 1", @cl_rm$;
	return;

// Starter kits: each of your companions drafted with the app's "Companions
// start unequipped" (the engine's row says so), here and standing, that
// hasn't had its chance yet (mod store, char scope, kit.c<char id>).
L_KitCheck:
	.@n = query_sql("SELECT name FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + getcharid(0) + " AND start_unequipped = 1", .@nm$);
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@ix = inarray(@cl_nm$, .@nm$[.@k]);
		if (.@ix < 0)
			continue;
		if (@cl_hp[.@ix] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (modstore_get("char", "kit.c" + @cl_cid[.@ix], 0))
			continue;
		callsub L_Kit, .@ix;
	}
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) (index) gets the kit for its class and level, once:
// only if it wears nothing yet (you may have dressed it first). Each piece
// goes into its bag and on, as a trade does; a companion that started
// unequipped counts all it wears as yours (the engine), so the Gear tab's
// Take back and our Bag tab both hand it to you, and nothing grows back.
L_Kit:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@c = @cl_cid[.@i];
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	modstore_set "char", "kit.c" + .@c, 1;	// its one chance, whatever happens next
	setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES, EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R;
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@ei); .@s++) {
		if (getequipid(.@ei[.@s], .@c) > 0)
			return;
	}
	.@lv = readparam(BaseLevel, .@c);
	.@t = 0;
	if (.@lv >= 70)
		.@t = 3;
	else if (.@lv >= 40)
		.@t = 2;
	else if (.@lv >= 20)
		.@t = 1;
	// The base class: transcendent and baby folded in, a Peco Knight is a Knight.
	.@job = roclass(eaclass(readparam(Class, .@c)) & ~(EAJL_UPPER | EAJL_BABY), readparam(Sex, .@c));
	.@v$ = ".kit_" + .@job + "_" + .@t;
	.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
	if (!.@n)
		return;
	.@got$ = "";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < .@n; .@k++) {
		.@id = getd(.@v$ + "[" + .@k + "]");
		if (getiteminfo(.@id, ITEMINFO_EQUIPLEVELMIN) > .@lv)
			continue;	// not yet (a low companion skips the odd piece)
		getitem .@id, 1, .@aid;
		equip .@id, .@c;
		.@got$ += (.@got$ == "" ? "" : ", ") + getitemname(.@id);
	}
	.@am = getd(".kitammo_" + .@job + "_" + .@t);
	if (.@am > 0) {
		getitem .@am, 500, .@aid;
		equip .@am, .@c;
		.@got$ += ", 500 " + getitemname(.@am);
	}
	// Our record of what it wears from you, so the Bag tab shows it and takes
	// it back (as gear dropped on it there).
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
	getinventorylist .@c;
	.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
		.@eq = getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c);
		if (!.@eq || .@eq == EQP_AMMO)
			continue;
		.@rec$ += (.@rec$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@eq + "," + getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c) + ",0";
	}
	if (.@rec$ != "")
		modstore_set "char", "dg.c" + .@c, .@rec$;
	if (.@got$ != "")
		dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " got a starter kit: " + .@got$ + ".", 0xAADDFF;
	return;

// A gift from companion getarg(0) (index) to you (attached), getarg(1) the moment:
// "" a quiet one, "mvp", "lvl" or "ann". At most one gift every 4 minutes, and
// from the same companion every 30. 1 when it gave something.
L_Gift:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@k$ = getarg(1);
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	if (!.gifts || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || !unitexists(.@aid))
		return 0;
	if (.@sec < @cl_gall || .@sec < getd("@cl_gcd_" + .@aid))
		return 0;
	.@ar = @cl_ar[.@i];
	// Rare and very rare, in tenths of a percent; better at the big moments.
	.@rr = 120;
	.@vr = 5;
	if (.@k$ == "mvp") {
		.@rr = 300;
		.@vr = 20;
	} else if (.@k$ != "") {
		.@rr = 200;
		.@vr = 10;
	}
	// Bonds (3.7.0): from Trusted on, half again the chance of a rare gift, and twice
	// the very rare.
	if (.mem) {
		if (callsub(L_BondTier, callsub(L_BondPts, @cl_cid[.@i])) >= 3) {
			.@rr = .@rr * 3 / 2;
			.@vr = .@vr * 2;
		}
	}
	.@roll = rand(1000);
	if (.@roll < .@vr) {
		.@e$ = "616:1:1";	// Old Card Album
		.@cat$ = "giftv";
	} else {
		.@tb$ = getd(".gc_" + .@ar + "$");
		.@cat$ = "giftc";
		if (.@roll < .@vr + .@rr) {
			.@tb$ = getd(".gr_" + .@ar + "$");
			.@cat$ = "giftr";
		}
		if (.@tb$ == "")
			return 0;
		explode(.@ent$, .@tb$, ",");
		.@e$ = .@ent$[rand(getarraysize(.@ent$))];
	}
	explode(.@p$, .@e$, ":");
	.@id = atoi(.@p$[0]);
	.@lo = atoi(.@p$[1]);
	.@hi = atoi(.@p$[2]);
	.@n = .@lo;
	if (.@hi > .@lo)
		.@n = rand(.@lo, .@hi);
	if (.@id == 0) {
		if (Zeny + .@n > MAX_ZENY)
			return 0;
		Zeny += .@n;
		.@what$ = .@n + " zeny";
	} else {
		if (!checkweight(.@id, .@n))
			return 0;	// you can't carry it: no gift this time
		getitem .@id, .@n;
		.@what$ = getitemname(.@id);
		if (.@n > 1)
			.@what$ = .@n + " " + .@what$;
	}
	setd "@cl_gcd_" + .@aid, .@sec + 1800;
	@cl_gall = .@sec + 240;
	callsub L_GiftWalk, .@i;
	// What it says: about the moment half the time, else about the gift.
	.@lc$ = .@cat$;
	if (.@k$ != "" && .@cat$ != "giftv" && rand(2))
		.@lc$ = "gift" + .@k$;
	.@t$ = replacestr(callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, .@lc$, .@ar), .@i, ""), "{i}", .@what$);
	.@em = ET_DELIGHT;
	if (.@cat$ == "giftv")
		.@em = ET_THROB;
	else if (.@id == 0)
		.@em = ET_MONEY;
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = .@aid;
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = .@em;
	dispbottom "[Companions] " + @cl_nm$[.@i] + " gave you " + .@what$ + ".", 0xFFCC66;
	if (.mem)
		modstore_inc("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".gifts", 1);
	return 1;

// Companion getarg(0) walks over to stand next to you (on your side facing it),
// when it's on your map and within 12 cells.
L_GiftWalk:
	.@aid = @cl_aid[getarg(0)];
	getmapxy(.@om$, .@ox, .@oy, BL_PC);
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != .@om$)
		return;
	.@dx = .@cx - .@ox;
	.@dy = .@cy - .@oy;
	if (max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) > 12 || max(.@dx, -.@dx, .@dy, -.@dy) <= 1)
		return;
	.@gx = .@ox + (.@dx > 0) - (.@dx < 0);
	.@gy = .@oy + (.@dy > 0) - (.@dy < 0);
	if (!checkcell(.@om$, .@gx, .@gy, cell_chkpass))
		return;
	callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, .@om$, .@cx, .@cy, .@gx, .@gy, .@ox, .@oy, 4000;
	return;

// Whether companion getarg(0) (an index of the tick's @cl_ arrays) roams.
L_Roams:
	return (inarray(@cl_rm$, @cl_nm$[getarg(0)]) >= 0);

// 2.5.6: roaming companions don't sit down to rest. The engine rests a companion
// before its strategy rules get a turn (population_engine.cpp: pop_shell_rest), so a
// rule can't keep it up; the only switch is "@companion rest <below> <until>", which
// is for the whole party (0 = never). While any of yours roams, the thresholds are
// saved (char var cl_rest_saved$, the most common pair among your companions) and set
// to never; once none roams, they're put back. Survives a server restart.
L_RoamRest:
	if (!.roamhelp)
		return;
	.@cid = getcharid(0);
	query_sql "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@cid + " AND active = 1 AND roam = 1", .@r;
	if (.@r[0] > 0) {
		if (cl_rest_saved$ != "")
			return;
		query_sql "SELECT rest_below, rest_until, COUNT(*) AS c FROM cp_companion_persistence WHERE owner_char_id = " + .@cid
			+ " AND active = 1 GROUP BY rest_below, rest_until ORDER BY c DESC LIMIT 1", .@b, .@u, .@cnt;
		if (.@b[0] <= 0)
			return;	// already never resting: nothing to give back later
		cl_rest_saved$ = .@b[0] + "," + .@u[0];
		atcommand "@companion rest 0 " + .@u[0];
		return;
	}
	if (cl_rest_saved$ == "")
		return;
	explode(.@p$, cl_rest_saved$, ",");
	cl_rest_saved$ = "";
	atcommand "@companion rest " + .@p$[0] + " " + .@p$[1];
	return;

// The highest outfit colour offered for a class. Built on the engine's own
// table (population_engine.cpp get_job_max_cloth_color, counted from kRO and
// iRO data, which re-rolls any colour above it at every summon), one lower:
// in play (2026-10-12) the top colour of every 0-4 class came out black and
// green. Knights and Crusaders always ride (the engine mounts them), and a
// dye tints the Peco too, which looked wrong: they keep the default.
L_ClothMax:
	.@j = getarg(0);
	setarray .@ride, Job_Knight, Job_Knight2, Job_Crusader, Job_Crusader2, Job_Lord_Knight, Job_Lord_Knight2,
		Job_Paladin, Job_Paladin2, Job_Baby_Knight, Job_Baby_Knight2, Job_Baby_Crusader, Job_Baby_Crusader2;
	if (inarray(.@ride, .@j) >= 0)
		return getbattleflag("min_cloth_color");
	setarray .@p3, Job_Assassin, Job_Crusader, Job_Crusader2, Job_Monk, Job_Sage, Job_Rogue, Job_Dancer,
		Job_Lord_Knight, Job_Lord_Knight2, Job_High_Priest, Job_High_Wizard, Job_Whitesmith, Job_Sniper,
		Job_Assassin_Cross, Job_Paladin, Job_Paladin2, Job_Champion, Job_Professor, Job_Stalker, Job_Creator,
		Job_Clown, Job_Gypsy, Job_Baby_Assassin, Job_Baby_Crusader, Job_Baby_Crusader2, Job_Baby_Monk,
		Job_Baby_Sage, Job_Baby_Rogue;
	setarray .@p4, Job_Swordman, Job_Mage, Job_Archer, Job_Acolyte, Job_Merchant, Job_Thief,
		Job_Knight, Job_Knight2, Job_Priest, Job_Wizard, Job_Blacksmith, Job_Hunter, Job_Alchemist, Job_Bard,
		Job_Swordman_High, Job_Mage_High, Job_Archer_High, Job_Acolyte_High, Job_Merchant_High, Job_Thief_High,
		Job_Baby_Swordman, Job_Baby_Mage, Job_Baby_Archer, Job_Baby_Acolyte, Job_Baby_Merchant, Job_Baby_Thief,
		Job_Baby_Knight, Job_Baby_Knight2, Job_Baby_Priest, Job_Baby_Wizard, Job_Baby_Blacksmith,
		Job_Baby_Hunter, Job_Baby_Alchemist, Job_Baby_Bard, Job_Baby_Dancer;
	.@max = getbattleflag("max_cloth_color");
	if (inarray(.@p3, .@j) >= 0)
		return min(3, .@max);
	if (inarray(.@p4, .@j) >= 0)
		return min(3, .@max);
	return .@max;

// Colours you picked (Bag tab), put back on a companion the engine summoned
// with its old ones (at login, after a map change, after a revive).
L_Dyes:
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		.@c = @cl_cid[.@i];
		.@p$ = "dye.c" + .@c;
		if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".cl")) {
			.@v = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".cl", 0);
			// A colour no longer offered (1.4.4), or a job change: the top one left.
			.@mx = callsub(L_ClothMax, readparam(Class, .@c));
			if (.@v > .@mx) {
				.@v = .@mx;
				modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".cl", .@v;
			}
			if (getlook(LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@c) != .@v)
				setlook LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
		} else if (getlook(LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@c) > callsub(L_ClothMax, readparam(Class, .@c))) {
			// Never dyed, but the engine rolled one of the colours that look broken
			// (or tint a Peco): the default instead, kept like a dye.
			.@v = getbattleflag("min_cloth_color");
			setlook LOOK_CLOTHES_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
			modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".cl", .@v;
		}
		if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".hc")) {
			.@v = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".hc", 0);
			if (getlook(LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@c) != .@v)
				setlook LOOK_HAIR_COLOR, .@v, .@c;
		}
	}
	return;

// A stack in companion getarg(0)'s bag, at getarg(1) in its @inventorylist_*
// (read just before), getarg(2) of it to you: refine, cards, random options
// and bound type come along.
L_ToYou:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	.@j = getarg(1);
	.@amt = getarg(2);
	.@id = getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c);
	.@ident = getvar(@inventorylist_identify[.@j], .@c);
	.@ref = getvar(@inventorylist_refine[.@j], .@c);
	.@attr = getvar(@inventorylist_attribute[.@j], .@c);
	.@c1 = getvar(@inventorylist_card1[.@j], .@c);
	.@c2 = getvar(@inventorylist_card2[.@j], .@c);
	.@c3 = getvar(@inventorylist_card3[.@j], .@c);
	.@c4 = getvar(@inventorylist_card4[.@j], .@c);
	.@bound = getvar(@inventorylist_bound[.@j], .@c);
	setarray .@oid, getvar(@inventorylist_option_id1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id2[.@j], .@c),
		getvar(@inventorylist_option_id3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_id5[.@j], .@c);
	setarray .@oval, getvar(@inventorylist_option_value1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value2[.@j], .@c),
		getvar(@inventorylist_option_value3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_value5[.@j], .@c);
	setarray .@opar, getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter1[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter2[.@j], .@c),
		getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter3[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter4[.@j], .@c), getvar(@inventorylist_option_parameter5[.@j], .@c);
	delitemidx getvar(@inventorylist_idx[.@j], .@c), .@amt, .@c;
	if (.@bound)
		getitembound3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@bound, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
	else
		getitem3 .@id, .@amt, .@ident, .@ref, .@attr, .@c1, .@c2, .@c3, .@c4, .@oid, .@oval, .@opar;
	return;

// Our record of gear dropped on companion getarg(0) (test build 2.3.0, mod
// store dg.c<char id>: "<slots>,<item id>,<its own item id>" split by ";").
// Entries for gear it no longer wears are dropped here (the engine changed
// it, or it was taken back some other way).
// L_DgMask: the slots in it. L_DgOwn: ",id,id," of its own pieces in its bag.
L_DgMask:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
	if (.@rec$ == "")
		return 0;
	setarray .@eb, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_SHOES,
		EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES,
		EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQI_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	explode(.@e$, .@rec$, ";");
	.@mask = 0;
	.@keep$ = "";
	for (.@r = 0; .@r < getarraysize(.@e$); .@r++) {
		if (.@e$[.@r] == "")
			continue;
		explode(.@f$, .@e$[.@r], ",");
		.@m = atoi(.@f$[0]);
		.@ok = 0;
		for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++) {
			if (.@m & .@eb[.@s]) {
				.@ok = getequipid(.@ei[.@s], .@c) == atoi(.@f$[1]);
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!.@ok)
			continue;
		.@mask = .@mask | .@m;
		.@keep$ += (.@keep$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@e$[.@r];
	}
	if (.@keep$ != .@rec$)
		callsub L_DgSave, .@c, .@keep$;
	return .@mask;

// Gear you dropped on companion getarg(0) (name getarg(1)) in slots getarg(2),
// from our record: off, back to you, and its own piece back on. Returns
// "OK|text", "ERR|text", or "" when no such gear is in the record.
L_DgBack:
	.@c = getarg(0);
	.@nm$ = getarg(1);
	.@eq = getarg(2);
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + .@c, "");
	if (.@rec$ == "")
		return "";
	explode(.@e$, .@rec$, ";");
	.@n = getarraysize(.@e$);
	for (.@r = 0; .@r < .@n; .@r++) {
		explode(.@f$, .@e$[.@r], ",");
		if (atoi(.@f$[0]) & .@eq)
			break;
	}
	if (.@r >= .@n)
		return "";
	.@m = atoi(.@f$[0]);
	.@id = atoi(.@f$[1]);
	.@own = atoi(.@f$[2]);
	// The record without this entry, kept whatever happens next.
	.@keep$ = "";
	for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@n; .@q++) {
		if (.@q != .@r && .@e$[.@q] != "")
			.@keep$ += (.@keep$ == "" ? "" : ";") + .@e$[.@q];
	}
	getinventorylist .@c;
	.@cnt = getvar(@inventorylist_count, .@c);
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < .@cnt; .@j++) {
		if (getvar(@inventorylist_id[.@j], .@c) == .@id && (getvar(@inventorylist_equip[.@j], .@c) & .@m))
			break;
	}
	if (.@j >= .@cnt) {
		callsub L_DgSave, .@c, .@keep$;
		return "ERR|" + .@nm$ + " isn't wearing that any more.";
	}
	if (!checkweight(.@id, 1))
		return "ERR|You can't carry the " + getitemname(.@id) + " it wears there.";
	setarray .@eb, EQP_HEAD_TOP, EQP_HEAD_MID, EQP_HEAD_LOW, EQP_ARMOR, EQP_HAND_R, EQP_HAND_L, EQP_GARMENT, EQP_SHOES,
		EQP_ACC_L, EQP_ACC_R, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQP_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQP_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	setarray .@ei, EQI_HEAD_TOP, EQI_HEAD_MID, EQI_HEAD_LOW, EQI_ARMOR, EQI_HAND_R, EQI_HAND_L, EQI_GARMENT, EQI_SHOES,
		EQI_ACC_L, EQI_ACC_R, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_TOP, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_MID, EQI_COSTUME_HEAD_LOW, EQI_COSTUME_GARMENT;
	for (.@s = 0; .@s < getarraysize(.@eb); .@s++) {
		if (.@m & .@eb[.@s]) {
			unequip .@ei[.@s], .@c;
			break;
		}
	}
	// Off now, still at the same index in its bag.
	callsub L_ToYou, .@c, .@j, 1;
	callsub L_DgSave, .@c, .@keep$;
	.@msg$ = "Took back the " + getitemname(.@id) + " from " + .@nm$ + ".";
	// (Two ifs: && reads both sides, and countitem(0) is an error.)
	if (.@own > 0) {
		if (countitem(.@own, getcharid(3, .@nm$))) {
			equip .@own, .@c;
			.@msg$ += " It put its " + getitemname(.@own) + " back on.";
		}
	}
	return "OK|" + .@msg$;

// Save the record getarg(1) for companion getarg(0); an empty one is removed.
L_DgSave:
	if (getarg(1) == "")
		modstore_delete "char", "dg.c" + getarg(0);
	else
		modstore_set "char", "dg.c" + getarg(0), getarg(1);
	return;

L_DgOwn:
	.@rec$ = modstore_get("char", "dg.c" + getarg(0), "");
	.@out$ = ",";
	if (.@rec$ == "")
		return .@out$;
	explode(.@e$, .@rec$, ";");
	for (.@r = 0; .@r < getarraysize(.@e$); .@r++) {
		explode(.@f$, .@e$[.@r], ",");
		if (atoi(.@f$[2]) > 0)
			.@out$ += .@f$[2] + ",";
	}
	return .@out$;

// "@@reply <request> <part>/<parts> <text>", 180 characters a line.
L_BagReply:
	.@rt$ = getarg(1);
	.@rl = getstrlen(.@rt$);
	.@rn = max(1, (.@rl + 179) / 180);
	for (.@rk = 0; .@rk < .@rn; .@rk++) {
		.@part$ = "";
		if (.@rl > 0)
			.@part$ = substr(.@rt$, .@rk * 180, min(.@rl, (.@rk + 1) * 180) - 1);
		dispbottom "@@reply " + getarg(0) + " " + (.@rk + 1) + "/" + .@rn + " " + .@part$;
	}
	return;

// The window's separators out of a text.
L_Clean:
	return replacestr(replacestr(getarg(0), "|", "/"), "~", "-");

// Memories: a companion seen for the first time is "met" here and now; one
// already known may have an anniversary today.
L_MemSeen:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
	if (!modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".met")) {
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".met", gettimetick(2);
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".metmap", strcharinfo(3);
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".metlv", BaseLevel;
		return;
	}
	.@days = (gettimetick(2) - modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", gettimetick(2))) / 86400;
	.@ann = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".ann", 0);
	setarray .@ms[0], 730, 365, 182, 100, 30, 7;
	// 3.0.1: said in words ("A month together already"), not a count of days.
	setarray .@mw$[0], "Two years", "A whole year", "Half a year", "A hundred days", "A month", "A week";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 6; .@k++) {
		if (.@days >= .@ms[.@k]) {
			if (.@ms[.@k] > .@ann && @cl_hp[.@i] > 0) {
				modstore_set "char", .@p$ + ".ann", .@ms[.@k];
				callsub L_QueueFmt, .@i, "anniv", "", ET_CONGRATULATION, .@days, .@mw$[.@k];
				if (rand(100) < 50)
					callsub L_Gift, .@i, "ann";
			}
			break;
		}
	}
	return;

// Memories: a place visited together for the first time. Instance maps
// (1@tower and the like) don't fit a store key and aren't counted.
L_MemMap:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	if (compare(.@m$, "@") || compare(.@m$, "-") || compare(.@m$, "."))
		return;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i] + ".maps";
		if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + "." + .@m$))
			continue;
		modstore_set "char", .@p$ + "." + .@m$, 1;	// counted for the Memories tab only (3.0.1)
	}
	return;

// A quiet thought out loud (3.0.1, replaces reciting stats): a "did you know" about
// where you are (or anywhere), a small shared moment, or now and then an easter egg.
// The last few of each aren't repeated (L_PickConv's list, @cl_rec$). 1 if one was said.
L_Thought:
	.@a = callsub(L_Pick, -1, -1);
	if (.@a < 0)
		return 0;
	.@ar = @cl_ar[.@a];
	.@r = rand(100);
	if (.@r < 5) {
		.@t$ = callsub(L_PickConv, "segg");
	} else if (.@r < 55) {
		.@t$ = callsub(L_PickLore, callsub(L_LoreRegion, strcharinfo(3)));
		if (.@t$ != "")
			.@t$ = callsub(L_Line, "dyk", .@ar) + .@t$;
	} else {
		// {m}: the town where you met, by name; lines that need it are passed over
		// when you met in a field or dungeon.
		.@m$ = callsub(L_TownName, modstore_get("char", "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@a] + ".metmap", ""));
		.@t$ = "";
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < 6; .@k++) {
			.@t$ = callsub(L_PickConv, "mom_" + .@ar);
			if (.@m$ != "" || !compare(.@t$, "{m}"))
				break;
		}
		if (.@m$ == "" && compare(.@t$, "{m}"))
			return 0;
		.@t$ = replacestr(.@t$, "{m}", .@m$);
	}
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return 0;
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, .@t$, .@a, "");
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@a];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@a];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = 0;
	return 1;

// A "did you know" fact for region getarg(0): one about that region 60% of the time
// (when there is one), else one that fits anywhere. The last 20 aren't repeated.
L_PickLore:
	.@rg$ = getarg(0);
	.@n = getarraysize(.lore$);
	.@want$ = "any";
	if (.@rg$ != "" && rand(100) < 60)
		.@want$ = .@rg$;
	.@k = 0;
	for (.@pass = 0; .@pass < 3 && !.@k; .@pass++) {
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@n; .@i++) {
			if (!compare(.lore$[.@i], .@want$ + "|") || inarray(@cl_lore, .@i) >= 0)
				continue;
			.@c[.@k] = .@i;
			.@k++;
		}
		if (!.@k) {
			if (.@want$ != "any")
				.@want$ = "any";	// nothing left for here: anywhere
			else
				deletearray @cl_lore;	// heard them all: start over
		}
	}
	if (!.@k)
		return "";
	.@i = .@c[0];
	if (.@k > 1)
		.@i = .@c[rand(.@k)];
	if (getarraysize(@cl_lore) >= 20)
		deletearray @cl_lore[0], 1;
	@cl_lore[getarraysize(@cl_lore)] = .@i;
	explode(.@part$, .lore$[.@i], "|");
	return .@part$[1];

// The lore region of map getarg(0): a code .lore$ uses, or "".
L_LoreRegion:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	setarray .@pf$, "prt_maze", "prt_sewb", "prontera", "prt_", "izlude", "iz_", "geffen", "gef_", "orcsdun", "payon", "pay_", "morocc", "moc_", "in_sphinx", "anthell",
		"alberta", "alb", "treasure", "tur_dun", "aldebaran", "alde_", "c_tower", "mjolnir_", "mjo_", "yuno", "juperos", "jupe_", "comodo", "cmd_", "beach_dun", "umbala", "um_",
		"xmas", "niflheim", "nif_", "glast_01", "gl_", "einbroch", "einbech", "ein_", "lighthalzen", "lhz_", "hugel", "hu_", "odin_tem", "rachel", "ra_", "amatsu", "ama_";
	setarray .@rg$, "prt", "prt", "prt", "prt", "izl", "izl", "gef", "gef", "gef", "pay", "pay", "moc", "moc", "moc", "moc",
		"alb", "alb", "alb", "alb", "alde", "alde", "alde", "mjo", "mjo", "yuno", "yuno", "yuno", "cmd", "cmd", "cmd", "um", "um",
		"xmas", "nif", "nif", "gl", "gl", "ein", "ein", "ein", "lhz", "lhz", "hu", "hu", "hu", "ra", "ra", "ama", "ama";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@pf$); .@i++) {
		if (compare(.@m$, .@pf$[.@i]))
			return .@rg$[.@i];
	}
	return "";

// A town's name for a map name, or "" if it isn't one of the towns.
L_TownName:
	setarray .@tm$, "prontera", "izlude", "geffen", "payon", "morocc", "alberta", "aldebaran", "yuno", "comodo", "umbala", "amatsu", "gonryun", "louyang", "ayothaya", "xmas", "niflheim", "einbroch", "einbech", "lighthalzen", "hugel", "rachel", "veins", "moscovia", "pay_arche";
	setarray .@tn$, "Prontera", "Izlude", "Geffen", "Payon", "Morroc", "Alberta", "Al De Baran", "Juno", "Comodo", "Umbala", "Amatsu", "Kunlun", "Luoyang", "Ayothaya", "Lutie", "Niflheim", "Einbroch", "Einbech", "Lighthalzen", "Hugel", "Rachel", "Veins", "Moscovia", "Archer Village";
	.@i = inarray(.@tm$, getarg(0));
	if (.@i < 0)
		return "";
	return .@tn$[.@i];

// Like L_QueueIdx, with {n} = getarg(4) and {m} = getarg(5) filled in first.
L_QueueFmt:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Line, getarg(1), @cl_ar[.@i]);
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return;
	.@t$ = replacestr(replacestr(.@t$, "{n}", "" + getarg(4)), "{m}", getarg(5));
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, .@t$, .@i, getarg(2));
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@i];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = getarg(3);
	return;

// Companions on your map, into @cl_* arrays (index 0..@cl_n-1).
L_Gather:
	@cl_n = 0;
	deletearray @cl_aid;
	deletearray @cl_cid;
	deletearray @cl_nm$;
	deletearray @cl_hp;
	deletearray @cl_mhp;
	deletearray @cl_sp;
	deletearray @cl_msp;
	deletearray @cl_ar;
	deletearray @cl_lv;
	deletearray @cl_cls;
	.@pid = getcharid(1);
	if (!.@pid)
		return;
	getpartymember .@pid, 1;
	.@cnt = $@partymembercount;
	if (.@cnt <= 0)
		return;
	copyarray .@cid[0], $@partymembercid[0], .@cnt;
	getpartymember .@pid, 2;
	copyarray .@aid[0], $@partymemberaid[0], .@cnt;
	getpartymember .@pid, 0;
	copyarray .@nm$[0], $@partymembername$[0], .@cnt;
	// 3.0.1: the client colours companions' lines in the chat box (setting chat_colour)
	// and learns who they are from this, sent when the names change or after a map change.
	.@all$ = "";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
		if (.@cid[.@i] >= 95000000)
			.@all$ += .@nm$[.@i] + "|";
	}
	if (.@all$ != @cl_names$) {
		@cl_names$ = .@all$;
		dispbottom "@@event clnames " + .@all$;
	}
	.@map$ = strcharinfo(3);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < .@cnt; .@i++) {
		if (.@cid[.@i] < 95000000)
			continue;	// a real player, not a companion
		.@hp = readparam(Hp, .@cid[.@i]);
		if (.@hp < 0)
			continue;	// not loaded
		if (strcharinfo(3, .@cid[.@i]) != .@map$)
			continue;	// left behind on another map
		@cl_aid[@cl_n] = .@aid[.@i];
		@cl_cid[@cl_n] = .@cid[.@i];
		@cl_nm$[@cl_n] = .@nm$[.@i];
		@cl_hp[@cl_n] = .@hp;
		@cl_mhp[@cl_n] = readparam(MaxHp, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_sp[@cl_n] = readparam(Sp, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_msp[@cl_n] = readparam(MaxSp, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_cls[@cl_n] = readparam(Class, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_lv[@cl_n] = readparam(BaseLevel, .@cid[.@i]);
		@cl_ar[@cl_n] = callsub(L_Arch, @cl_cls[@cl_n]);
		@cl_n++;
	}
	// 3.2.0: the client's portraits (client/portraits.js) need each
	// one's class and sex, "Name:class:sex|" (sex 0 female, 1 male).
	if (.portraits) {
		.@cast$ = "";
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++)
			.@cast$ += @cl_nm$[.@i] + ":" + @cl_cls[.@i] + ":" + readparam(Sex, @cl_cid[.@i]) + "|";
		if (.@cast$ != @cl_cast$) {
			@cl_cast$ = .@cast$;
			dispbottom "@@event clcast " + .@cast$;
		}
	}
	return;

// Personality from the class family.
L_Arch:
	.@ea = eaclass(getarg(0));
	.@b = .@ea & EAJ_BASEMASK;
	if (.@b == EAJ_SWORDMAN)
		return 1;
	if (.@b == EAJ_MAGE)
		return 2;
	if (.@b == EAJ_ARCHER || .@b == EAJ_GUNSLINGER)
		return 3;
	if (.@b == EAJ_ACOLYTE) {
		if (.@ea & EAJL_2_2)
			return 5;	// Monk line
		return 4;
	}
	if (.@b == EAJ_TAEKWON) {
		if (.@ea & EAJL_2_2)
			return 2;	// Soul Linker
		return 5;
	}
	if (.@b == EAJ_MERCHANT)
		return 7;
	if (.@b == EAJ_THIEF || .@b == EAJ_NINJA)
		return 6;
	return 0;

// A living companion: getarg(0) index to skip (-1 none), getarg(1)
// personality to prefer (-1 none). Returns an index or -1.
L_Pick:
	.@ex = getarg(0);
	.@pref = getarg(1);
	.@k = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (.@i == .@ex || @cl_hp[.@i] <= 0)
			continue;
		if (.@pref >= 0 && @cl_ar[.@i] == .@pref)
			return .@i;
		.@c[.@k] = .@i;
		.@k++;
	}
	if (!.@k)
		return -1;
	if (.@k == 1)
		return .@c[0];	// rand(1) logs an error on this server
	return .@c[rand(.@k)];

// A random line for category getarg(0), personality getarg(1).
L_Line:
	.@v$ = "." + getarg(0) + "_" + getarg(1) + "$";
	.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
	if (!.@n) {
		.@v$ = "." + getarg(0) + "_0$";
		.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
		if (!.@n)
			return "";
	}
	if (.@n == 1)
		return getd(.@v$ + "[0]");
	return getd(.@v$ + "[" + rand(.@n) + "]");

// Fill {t} (companion getarg(1)'s title for you) and {a} (getarg(2)).
L_Text:
	.@t$ = replacestr(getarg(0), "{t}", .ttl$[@cl_cid[getarg(1)] % .nt]);
	return replacestr(.@t$, "{a}", getarg(2));

// Companion getarg(0) says a line of category getarg(1) now.
L_SpeakNow:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, getarg(1), @cl_ar[.@i]), .@i, getarg(2));
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return;
	callsub L_SayAid, @cl_aid[.@i], @cl_nm$[.@i], .@t$;
	if (getarg(3))
		callsub L_EmoteIdx, .@i, getarg(3);
	@cl_talk = gettimetick(2) + 6;
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) says a line of category getarg(1) at its turn.
L_QueueIdx:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@t$ = callsub(L_Text, callsub(L_Line, getarg(1), @cl_ar[.@i]), .@i, getarg(2));
	if (.@t$ == "")
		return;
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@i];
	@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@i];
	@cl_qt$[.@q] = .@t$;
	@cl_qe[.@q] = getarg(3);
	return;

// Two companions (getarg(0), getarg(1)) talk; a third joins sometimes.
// getarg(2) the map, getarg(3) "night" / "morning" / "". What about:
//   the first time they become friends or close friends: that moment;
//   else, by chance: all three; their two personalities; the place, the
//   hour or a boss just beaten; a memory; how well they know each other;
//   anything (.conv$). The last 12 conversations aren't repeated.
// How well they know each other is the number of talks they've had,
// kept in the store (r.c<low char id>_<high char id>).
L_Conversation:
	.@a = getarg(0);
	.@b = getarg(1);
	.@c = -1;
	.@d = -1;
	if (@cl_n >= 3) {
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < 5 && .@c < 0; .@k++) {
			.@x = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
			if (.@x >= 0 && .@x != .@b)
				.@c = .@x;
		}
	}
	if (@cl_n >= 4 && .@c >= 0) {
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < 8 && .@d < 0; .@k++) {
			.@x = callsub(L_Pick, .@a, -1);
			if (.@x >= 0 && .@x != .@b && .@x != .@c)
				.@d = .@x;
		}
	}
	// How the topic is chosen (by a roll under 100): a small party mostly
	// talks in pairs; with three or more companions the whole group joins in.
	// 3.0.0: what's left after friends (anything, .conv$) got bigger with the new lines.
	setarray .@th, 0, 15, 35, 52, 60, 72;	// four, three, pair, place, memory, friends
	if (@cl_n >= 3)
		setarray .@th, 20, 42, 55, 66, 72, 80;
	if (.@d < 0)
		.@th[0] = 0;
	.@rk$ = callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@a], @cl_cid[.@b]);
	.@fam = modstore_inc("char", .@rk$, 1);
	if (.@fam <= 0)
		.@fam = 10;	// the store refused: treat them as friends
	.@n$ = "";
	.@m$ = "";
	.@cat$ = "";
	if (.@fam == 6)
		.@cat$ = "tu1";
	else if (.@fam == 20)
		.@cat$ = "tu2";
	else {
		.@r = rand(100);
		if (.@d >= 0 && .@r < .@th[0])
			.@cat$ = "quad";
		// 3.0.0: about one in five is an easter egg: an isekai nod, a bit of
		// Rune-Midgard lore, or a what-if about the world and its NPCs.
		if (.@cat$ == "" && rand(100) < 20) {
			setarray .@eg$, "egg_isekai", "egg_lore", "egg_npc";
			.@cat$ = .@eg$[rand(3)];
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@c >= 0 && .@r < .@th[1])
			.@cat$ = "trio";
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[2]) {
			// their pair, lower personality speaks first
			.@pa = @cl_ar[.@a];
			.@pb = @cl_ar[.@b];
			if (.@pa > .@pb) {
				.@x = .@a; .@a = .@b; .@b = .@x;
				.@x = .@pa; .@pa = .@pb; .@pb = .@x;
			}
			if (getarraysize(getd(".pc_" + .@pa + "_" + .@pb + "$")))
				.@cat$ = "pc_" + .@pa + "_" + .@pb;
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[3]) {
			.@nc = 0;
			if (@cl_lastmvp && gettimetick(2) - @cl_lastmvp < 900) {
				.@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_boss"; .@nc++;
				.@m$ = @cl_lastmvp$;
			}
			if (getarg(3) != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_" + getarg(3); .@nc++; }
			.@rg$ = callsub(L_Region, getarg(2));
			if (.@rg$ != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_" + .@rg$; .@nc++; }
			.@mk$ = callsub(L_MapKind, getarg(2));
			if (.@mk$ != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cx_" + .@mk$; .@nc++; }
			if (.@nc) {
				.@cat$ = .@cx$[0];
				if (.@nc > 1)
					.@cat$ = .@cx$[rand(.@nc)];
			}
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[4] && .mem) {
			.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@a];
			.@nc = 0;
			if (modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", "") != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cm_mvp"; .@nc++; }
			// 3.0.1: no counts (kills, days); where they met only when it was a town.
			.@town$ = callsub(L_TownName, modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".metmap", ""));
			if (.@town$ != "") { .@cx$[.@nc] = "cm_met"; .@nc++; }
			if (.@nc) {
				.@cat$ = .@cx$[0];
				if (.@nc > 1)
					.@cat$ = .@cx$[rand(.@nc)];
				if (.@cat$ == "cm_mvp") .@m$ = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".firstmvp", "");
				else .@m$ = .@town$;
			}
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "" && .@r < .@th[5]) {
			.@cat$ = "t0";
			if (.@fam >= 20) .@cat$ = "t2";
			else if (.@fam >= 6) .@cat$ = "t1";
		}
		if (.@cat$ == "")
			.@cat$ = "conv";
	}
	.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, .@cat$);
	if (.@line$ == "")
		.@line$ = callsub(L_PickConv, "conv");
	if (.@cat$ != "quad")
		.@d = -1;
	if (.@cat$ != "trio" && .@cat$ != "quad")
		.@c = -1;
	// A group talk brings everyone in it closer, not only the first two.
	if (.@c >= 0) {
		callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@c;
		callsub L_Bump, .@b, .@c;
	}
	if (.@d >= 0) {
		callsub L_Bump, .@a, .@d;
		callsub L_Bump, .@b, .@d;
		callsub L_Bump, .@c, .@d;
	}
	callsub L_QueueConv, .@a, .@b, .@c, .@line$, .@n$, .@m$, .@d;
	return;

// One more talk between companions getarg(0) and getarg(1) (the store key
// L_Conversation keeps for each pair).
L_Bump:
	.@x = @cl_cid[getarg(0)];
	.@y = @cl_cid[getarg(1)];
	modstore_inc("char", "r.c" + min(.@x, .@y) + "_" + max(.@x, .@y), 1);
	return;

// A conversation of category getarg(0) (an array .<cat>$) that isn't one
// of the last 12 used, or "" when the category has none.
L_PickConv:
	.@v$ = "." + getarg(0) + "$";
	.@n = getarraysize(getd(.@v$));
	if (!.@n)
		return "";
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 6; .@k++) {
		.@i = 0;
		if (.@n > 1)
			.@i = rand(.@n);
		if (inarray(@cl_rec$, getarg(0) + "#" + .@i) < 0)
			break;
	}
	if (getarraysize(@cl_rec$) >= 12)
		deletearray @cl_rec$[0], 1;
	@cl_rec$[getarraysize(@cl_rec$)] = getarg(0) + "#" + .@i;
	return getd(.@v$ + "[" + .@i + "]");

// snow / desert / sea / forest, or "".
L_Region:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	if (compare(.@m$, "xmas") || compare(.@m$, "ice_dun"))
		return "snow";
	if (compare(.@m$, "moc_") || compare(.@m$, "morocc") || compare(.@m$, "sphinx"))
		return "desert";
	if (compare(.@m$, "alberta") || compare(.@m$, "alb_") || compare(.@m$, "izlude") || compare(.@m$, "iz_") || compare(.@m$, "beach") || compare(.@m$, "comodo") || compare(.@m$, "cmd_"))
		return "sea";
	if (compare(.@m$, "pay_") || compare(.@m$, "payon") || compare(.@m$, "um_") || compare(.@m$, "umbala"))
		return "forest";
	return "";

// Queue conversation getarg(3) between companions getarg(0) (A), getarg(1)
// (B), getarg(2) (C, -1 for none) and getarg(6) (D, -1 or left out for none),
// with {n} = getarg(4), {m} = getarg(5).
L_QueueConv:
	.@a = getarg(0);
	.@b = getarg(1);
	.@c = getarg(2);
	.@d = getarg(6, -1);
	.@line$ = replacestr(replacestr(getarg(3), "{n}", getarg(4)), "{m}", getarg(5));
	.@line$ = replacestr(replacestr(.@line$, "{A}", @cl_nm$[.@a]), "{B}", @cl_nm$[.@b]);
	if (.@c >= 0)
		.@line$ = replacestr(.@line$, "{C}", @cl_nm$[.@c]);
	if (.@d >= 0)
		.@line$ = replacestr(.@line$, "{D}", @cl_nm$[.@d]);
	explode(.@turn$, .@line$, "^");
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_qa);
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@turn$); .@i++) {
		.@s$ = charat(.@turn$[.@i], 0);
		.@who = .@a;
		.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@b];
		if (.@s$ == "B") {
			.@who = .@b;
			.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@a];
		} else if (.@s$ == "C" && .@c >= 0) {
			.@who = .@c;
			.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@a];
		} else if (.@s$ == "D" && .@d >= 0) {
			.@who = .@d;
			.@other$ = @cl_nm$[.@a];
		}
		@cl_qa[.@q] = @cl_aid[.@who];
		@cl_qn$[.@q] = @cl_nm$[.@who];
		@cl_qt$[.@q] = callsub(L_Text, delchar(.@turn$[.@i], 0), .@who, .@other$);
		@cl_qe[.@q] = 0;
		.@q++;
	}
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) strolls 1-3 cells to a free spot 2-4 cells from you
// (you at getarg(2), getarg(3) on map getarg(1)). The engine's follow and
// the spacing_* rules' Hold would stop the walk, so the
// companion gets Entry_Queue_Apply_Delay for 2.5 s: a battleground queue
// timer that does nothing by itself, which the spacing Hold waits out.
L_Wander:
	.@aid = @cl_aid[getarg(0)];
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(1))
		return;
	.@ox = getarg(2);
	.@oy = getarg(3);
	// As far as the party spacing lets them stand (.near); a scout (Wanderer)
	// checks one cell further out.
	.@far = .near;
	if (@cl_ar[getarg(0)] == 3)
		.@far = .near + 1;
	.@dmin = 2;
	if (.near <= 2)
		.@dmin = 1;
	// Where the others stand, so nobody walks onto someone.
	.@nb = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < @cl_n; .@k++) {
		if (@cl_aid[.@k] != .@aid && getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@k]) == 0) {
			.@bx[.@nb] = .@px;
			.@by[.@nb] = .@py;
			.@nb++;
		}
	}
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < 10; .@k++) {
		.@x = .@ox + rand(-.@far, .@far);
		.@y = .@oy + rand(-.@far, .@far);
		.@do = max(.@x - .@ox, .@ox - .@x, .@y - .@oy, .@oy - .@y);
		.@dc = max(.@x - .@cx, .@cx - .@x, .@y - .@cy, .@cy - .@y);
		if (.@do < .@dmin || .@dc < 1 || .@dc > max(1, .@far - 1))
			continue;
		if (!checkcell(getarg(1), .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
			continue;
		for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@nb; .@q++) {
			if (.@bx[.@q] == .@x && .@by[.@q] == .@y)
				break;
		}
		if (.@q < .@nb)
			continue;	// taken
		sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, 2500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
		// A stroll the engine cut short (a fight, catching up with you) leaves
		// rAthena's forced-walk flag set until the unit next arrives somewhere,
		// and unitwalk refuses it till then: clear it first (quietly).
		unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitwalk .@aid, .@x, .@y;
		if (@cl_ar[getarg(0)] == 3) {
			// A scout looks about, and now and then reports.
			if (rand(100) < 15 && gettimetick(2) >= @cl_talk)
				callsub L_SpeakNow, getarg(0), "patrol", "", ET_STARE_ABOUT;
			else if (rand(100) < 25)
				callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_STARE_ABOUT;
		} else if (rand(100) < 20)
			callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_QUESTION;
		return;
	}
	return;

// Scout getarg(0) on its patrol round you (getarg(2), getarg(3) on map
// getarg(1)); the tick says when. Per scout: @cl_pw_<aid> the waypoint it
// went to (+1), @cl_pd_ the way round (1/-1), @cl_pl_ legs this lap, @cl_pn_
// the next leg (s), @cl_ps_ the stop (@cl_still) this patrol belongs to.
L_Patrol:
	.@aid = @cl_aid[getarg(0)];
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(1))
		return;
	.@ox = getarg(2);
	.@oy = getarg(3);
	.@r = .near + 1;
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	if (getd("@cl_ps_" + .@aid) != @cl_still) {
		// A new stop: the first leg goes to the waypoint nearest to it, then on
		// round either way.
		setd "@cl_ps_" + .@aid, @cl_still;
		.@bd = 999;
		.@bk = 0;
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < 8; .@k++) {
			.@wx = .@ox + .rx[.@k] * .@r;
			.@wy = .@oy + .ry[.@k] * .@r;
			.@d = max(.@wx - .@cx, .@cx - .@wx, .@wy - .@cy, .@cy - .@wy);
			if (.@d < .@bd) {
				.@bd = .@d;
				.@bk = .@k;
			}
		}
		.@dir = 1;
		if (rand(2))
			.@dir = -1;
		setd "@cl_pd_" + .@aid, .@dir;
		setd "@cl_pw_" + .@aid, (.@bk - .@dir + 8) % 8 + 1;
		setd "@cl_pl_" + .@aid, 0;
		setd "@cl_pn_" + .@aid, .@sec + 1 + rand(3);
		return;
	}
	if (.@sec < getd("@cl_pn_" + .@aid))
		return;
	.@dir = getd("@cl_pd_" + .@aid);
	if (getd("@cl_pl_" + .@aid) >= 8) {
		// A lap done: a look about, maybe a word, a rest, then round again
		// (sometimes the other way).
		setd "@cl_pl_" + .@aid, 0;
		setd "@cl_pn_" + .@aid, .@sec + 15 + rand(16);
		if (rand(100) < 40)
			setd "@cl_pd_" + .@aid, -.@dir;
		if (rand(100) < 20 && .@sec >= @cl_talk)
			callsub L_SpeakNow, getarg(0), "patrol", "", ET_STARE_ABOUT;
		else if (rand(100) < 35)
			callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_STARE_ABOUT;
		return;
	}
	// Where you and the others stand: a waypoint on someone is skipped.
	.@nb = 1;
	.@bx[0] = .@ox;
	.@by[0] = .@oy;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < @cl_n; .@k++) {
		if (@cl_aid[.@k] != .@aid && getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@k]) == 0) {
			.@bx[.@nb] = .@px;
			.@by[.@nb] = .@py;
			.@nb++;
		}
	}
	.@w = getd("@cl_pw_" + .@aid) - 1;
	for (.@t = 0; .@t < 3; .@t++) {
		.@w = (.@w + .@dir + 8) % 8;
		setd "@cl_pl_" + .@aid, getd("@cl_pl_" + .@aid) + 1;
		setd "@cl_pw_" + .@aid, .@w + 1;
		.@x = .@ox + .rx[.@w] * .@r;
		.@y = .@oy + .ry[.@w] * .@r;
		if (!checkcell(getarg(1), .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
			continue;	// a wall, water: on to the next one
		for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@nb; .@q++) {
			if (.@bx[.@q] == .@x && .@by[.@q] == .@y)
				break;
		}
		if (.@q < .@nb)
			continue;
		sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, 3500, 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
		unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;
		if (!unitwalk(.@aid, .@x, .@y))
			continue;	// no way there from here
		setd "@cl_pn_" + .@aid, .@sec + 3;
		if (rand(100) < 10)
			callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_STARE_ABOUT;
		return;
	}
	setd "@cl_pn_" + .@aid, .@sec + 3;
	return;

// One companion that has sat a while gets up for a moment (the tick says
// when): it moves to a new spot by its favourite (L_Fav), or turns to look at
// you, at whoever spoke last, at a friend or about. You at getarg(1),
// getarg(2) on map getarg(0). The idle_sit rule sits it down again once the
// marker from L_FaceWalk runs out.
L_Restless:
	.@sec = gettimetick(2);
	.@still = .@sec - @cl_still;
	.@ox = getarg(1);
	.@oy = getarg(2);
	.@w = 0;
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < @cl_n; .@i++) {
		if (@cl_hp[.@i] <= 0 || getd("@cl_rst_" + @cl_aid[.@i]) || callsub(L_Roams, .@i))
			continue;	// down, resting to get HP/SP back, or off roaming: leave it be
		.@thr = 60;
		if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 3)
			.@thr = 180;
		if (.@still < .@thr + 10)
			continue;	// not sitting yet
		if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 4 && .@sec - @cl_hurt_t < 60)
			continue;	// a Healer stays ready while someone's hurt
		.@wc[.@w] = .@i;
		.@w++;
	}
	if (!.@w)
		return;
	.@i = .@wc[0];
	if (.@w > 1)
		.@i = .@wc[rand(.@w)];	// rand(1) logs an error on this server
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(0))
		return;
	// Who it's fond of, and where they are.
	.@f = callsub(L_Fav, .@i);
	.@fx = .@ox;
	.@fy = .@oy;
	// (Two ifs: the script's && reads both sides, and @cl_aid[-1] when the
	// favourite is you stopped the tick with your character still attached,
	// which froze you: no walking, no NPCs, until a relog.)
	if (.@f >= 0) {
		if (getmapxy(.@fm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@f]) == 0) {
			.@fx = .@px;
			.@fy = .@py;
		}
	}
	stand @cl_nm$[.@i];

	// A new spot (not the Rogue, it stays where it sat): a free cell next to
	// its favourite, or anywhere near you, and it sits facing them.
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] != 6 && rand(100) < 40) {
		.@nb = 1;
		.@bx[0] = .@ox;
		.@by[0] = .@oy;
		for (.@k = 0; .@k < @cl_n; .@k++) {
			if (.@k == .@i)
				continue;
			if (getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@k]) == 0) {
				.@bx[.@nb] = .@px;
				.@by[.@nb] = .@py;
				.@nb++;
			}
			if (getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@k]) == @cl_still) {
				.@bx[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gx_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
				.@by[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gy_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
				.@nb++;
			}
		}
		.@nc = 0;
		for (.@t = 0; .@t < 20 && !.@nc; .@t++) {
			if (.@t < 8) {
				// next to its favourite: the 8 cells round them, from a varying start
				.@x = .@fx + .rx[(.@t + .@i) % 8];
				.@y = .@fy + .ry[(.@t + .@i) % 8];
			} else {
				.@x = .@ox + rand(-.near, .near);
				.@y = .@oy + rand(-.near, .near);
			}
			if ((.@x == .@cx && .@y == .@cy) || max(.@x - .@ox, .@ox - .@x, .@y - .@oy, .@oy - .@y) > .near)
				continue;
			if (!checkcell(getarg(0), .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
				continue;
			for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@nb; .@q++) {
				if (.@bx[.@q] == .@x && .@by[.@q] == .@y)
					break;
			}
			if (.@q < .@nb)
				continue;
			.@nc = 1;
		}
		if (.@nc) {
			setd "@cl_gd_" + .@aid, @cl_still;
			setd "@cl_gx_" + .@aid, .@x;
			setd "@cl_gy_" + .@aid, .@y;
			if (rand(100) < 30)
				callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, ET_SLEEPY;	// a stretch
			callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, getarg(0), .@cx, .@cy, .@x, .@y, .@fx, .@fy, 6000;
			return;
		}
	}

	// A glance: a step back and onto its spot again, which turns it to face
	// whoever spoke just now, else you, a friend, or somewhere out there.
	.@tx = .@ox;
	.@ty = .@oy;
	.@em = 0;
	.@r = rand(100);
	if (@cl_said && @cl_said != .@aid && gettimetick(0) - @cl_saidt < 10000
			&& getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_said) == 0 && .@pm$ == getarg(0)) {
		.@tx = .@px;
		.@ty = .@py;
	} else if (.@r < 50) {
		if (rand(100) < 30)
			.@em = ET_STARE;
	} else if (.@r < 80 && @cl_n > 1) {
		.@j = .@f;
		if (.@j < 0 || .@j == .@i) {
			.@j = 0;
			if (@cl_n > 2)
				.@j = rand(@cl_n - 1);	// rand(1) logs an error on this server
			if (.@j >= .@i)
				.@j++;
		}
		if (getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@j]) == 0) {
			.@tx = .@px;
			.@ty = .@py;
			if (rand(100) < 25)
				.@em = ET_SMILE;
		}
	} else {
		.@k = rand(8);
		.@tx = .@cx + .rx[.@k] * 5;
		.@ty = .@cy + .ry[.@k] * 5;
		.@em = ET_STARE_ABOUT;
	}
	if (.@em)
		callsub L_EmoteAid, .@aid, .@em;
	callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, getarg(0), .@cx, .@cy, .@cx, .@cy, .@tx, .@ty, 3000;
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) goes to sit by the one it is most comfortable with
// (L_Fav): a free cell next to them, within 4 cells of you (getarg(2),
// getarg(3) on map getarg(1)). Where it ends up is kept in @cl_gx_/gy_<aid>
// for the ones after it; the emote for the bond comes once it's there.
L_SitBy:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@aid = @cl_aid[.@i];
	.@ox = getarg(2);
	.@oy = getarg(3);
	if (getmapxy(.@cm$, .@cx, .@cy, BL_PC, .@aid) != 0 || .@cm$ != getarg(1))
		return;
	setd "@cl_gx_" + .@aid, .@cx;
	setd "@cl_gy_" + .@aid, .@cy;
	// The Rogue, a lone wolf: about a third of the time it sits apart, where it is.
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 6 && rand(100) < 35) {
		callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, ET_COOL;
		return;
	}
	.@f = callsub(L_Fav, .@i);
	.@em = callsub(L_BondEmote, .@i, .@f);
	if (.@f < 0) {
		.@tx = .@ox;
		.@ty = .@oy;
	} else {
		.@faid = @cl_aid[.@f];
		if (getd("@cl_gd_" + .@faid) == @cl_still) {
			// It already went to sit. If by me, I stay: it came to me.
			if (callsub(L_Fav, .@f) == .@i) {
				callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, .@em;
				return;
			}
			.@tx = getd("@cl_gx_" + .@faid);
			.@ty = getd("@cl_gy_" + .@faid);
		} else if (getmapxy(.@fm$, .@tx, .@ty, BL_PC, .@faid) != 0)
			return;
	}
	// Already beside them.
	if (max(.@cx - .@tx, .@tx - .@cx, .@cy - .@ty, .@ty - .@cy) == 1) {
		callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, .@em;
		return;
	}
	// Taken: where the others stand, and where the ones that went to sit went.
	.@nb = 0;
	for (.@k = 0; .@k < @cl_n; .@k++) {
		if (.@k == .@i)
			continue;
		if (getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC, @cl_aid[.@k]) == 0) {
			.@bx[.@nb] = .@px;
			.@by[.@nb] = .@py;
			.@nb++;
		}
		if (getd("@cl_gd_" + @cl_aid[.@k]) == @cl_still) {
			.@bx[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gx_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
			.@by[.@nb] = getd("@cl_gy_" + @cl_aid[.@k]);
			.@nb++;
		}
	}
	// The free cell next to them that is nearest to it.
	.@bd = 99;
	for (.@dx = -1; .@dx <= 1; .@dx++) {
		for (.@dy = -1; .@dy <= 1; .@dy++) {
			.@x = .@tx + .@dx;
			.@y = .@ty + .@dy;
			if ((.@dx == 0 && .@dy == 0) || (.@x == .@ox && .@y == .@oy))
				continue;
			if (max(.@x - .@ox, .@ox - .@x, .@y - .@oy, .@oy - .@y) > .near)
				continue;
			if (!checkcell(getarg(1), .@x, .@y, cell_chkpass))
				continue;
			for (.@q = 0; .@q < .@nb; .@q++) {
				if (.@bx[.@q] == .@x && .@by[.@q] == .@y)
					break;
			}
			if (.@q < .@nb)
				continue;
			.@d = max(.@x - .@cx, .@cx - .@x, .@y - .@cy, .@cy - .@y);
			if (.@d < .@bd) {
				.@bd = .@d;
				.@gx = .@x;
				.@gy = .@y;
			}
		}
	}
	if (.@bd == 99)
		return;	// no room next to them: it sits where it is
	setd "@cl_gx_" + .@aid, .@gx;
	setd "@cl_gy_" + .@aid, .@gy;
	// It sits down facing the one it came to sit with.
	callsub L_FaceWalk, .@aid, getarg(1), .@cx, .@cy, .@gx, .@gy, .@tx, .@ty, 5000;
	callsub L_EmoteLater, .@aid, .@em;
	return;

// Companion getarg(0) at getarg(2),getarg(3) on map getarg(1) walks to
// getarg(4),getarg(5) and ends up facing getarg(6),getarg(7). No script command
// turns a player-type unit, but one faces the way its last step went: so it
// walks to the cell just behind the spot first (seen from the target), and
// steps onto the spot on the next tick. Marker for getarg(8) ms, which holds
// off the spacing Hold and the idle_sit rule meanwhile.
L_FaceWalk:
	.@aid = getarg(0);
	.@gx = getarg(4);
	.@gy = getarg(5);
	.@sx = (getarg(6) > .@gx) - (getarg(6) < .@gx);
	.@sy = (getarg(7) > .@gy) - (getarg(7) < .@gy);
	.@px = .@gx - .@sx;
	.@py = .@gy - .@sy;
	sc_start SC_ENTRY_QUEUE_APPLY_DELAY, getarg(8), 1, 10000, SCSTART_NOAVOID|SCSTART_NOTICKDEF|SCSTART_NORATEDEF, .@aid;
	unitstopwalk .@aid, USW_FORCE_STOP;
	.@here = (.@gx == getarg(2) && .@gy == getarg(3));
	// Already right behind it, no facing wanted, or no room behind: straight
	// there (a glance on its own spot then just doesn't happen).
	if ((.@sx == 0 && .@sy == 0) || (.@px == getarg(2) && .@py == getarg(3))
			|| !checkcell(getarg(1), .@px, .@py, cell_chkpass)) {
		if (!.@here)
			unitwalk .@aid, .@gx, .@gy;
		return;
	}
	if (!unitwalk(.@aid, .@px, .@py)) {
		if (!.@here)
			unitwalk .@aid, .@gx, .@gy;
		return;
	}
	.@d = max(.@px - getarg(2), getarg(2) - .@px, .@py - getarg(3), getarg(3) - .@py);
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_fa);
	@cl_fa[.@q] = .@aid;
	@cl_fx[.@q] = .@gx;
	@cl_fy[.@q] = .@gy;
	@cl_ft[.@q] = gettimetick(0) + 400 + .@d * 200;
	@cl_fs[.@q] = @cl_still;
	return;

// An emote (getarg(1), 0 = none) from getarg(0) in about 4 s, once it got there.
L_EmoteLater:
	if (!getarg(1))
		return;
	.@q = getarraysize(@cl_pea);
	@cl_pea[.@q] = getarg(0);
	@cl_pee[.@q] = getarg(1);
	@cl_pet[.@q] = gettimetick(2) + 4;
	return;

// Bonds. Who companion getarg(0) is most comfortable with: -1 you, or the
// index of another companion here. Ties go to you, the leader.
//   with you:  5 (you hired it) + 1 a day together + 1 per 100 monsters
//              + 2 per MVP + 1 per 10 places seen (memories, m.c<id>.*);
//              Guardian x1.5 (its oath), Rookie x1.25 (looks up to you)
//   with another companion: 1 per talk they've shared (r.c<lo>_<hi>, as
//              their friendship); x1.5 when either is a Merchant (sociable)
//   a Healer: +5 to whoever was hurt last, in the last 5 minutes
L_Fav:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@best = -1;
	.@bv = callsub(L_PlayerBond, .@i);
	for (.@j = 0; .@j < @cl_n; .@j++) {
		if (.@j == .@i || @cl_hp[.@j] <= 0)
			continue;
		.@v = callsub(L_PairBond, .@i, .@j);
		if (.@v > .@bv) {
			.@bv = .@v;
			.@best = .@j;
		}
	}
	return .@best;

L_PlayerBond:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@p$ = "m.c" + @cl_cid[.@i];
	.@b = 5;
	if (modstore_exists("char", .@p$ + ".met"))
		.@b += (gettimetick(2) - modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", gettimetick(2))) / 86400;
	.@b += modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 0) / 100 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 0) * 2
		+ modstore_count("char", .@p$ + ".maps") / 10;
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 1)
		.@b = .@b * 3 / 2;
	else if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 0)
		.@b = .@b * 5 / 4;
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 4 && @cl_hurt_aid == getcharid(3) && gettimetick(2) - @cl_hurt_t < 300)
		.@b += 5;
	return .@b;

// Bonds (3.7.0): points from the memories of companion char id getarg(0). Days
// together 5, 20 kills 1, an MVP 10, a new place 2, a gift 5, a camp night 15,
// 10 minutes together 1. With the tiers in .bt_need, a party that played a week
// together is Trusted.
L_BondPts:
	.@p$ = "m.c" + getarg(0);
	.@pt = 0;
	.@met = modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".met", 0);
	if (.@met > 0)
		.@pt = (gettimetick(2) - .@met) / 86400 * 5;
	.@pt += modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".kills", 0) / 20 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mvps", 0) * 10
		+ modstore_count("char", .@p$ + ".maps") * 2 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".gifts", 0) * 5
		+ modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".camps", 0) * 15 + modstore_get("char", .@p$ + ".mins", 0) / 10;
	return .@pt;

// The tier (1 Acquaintance .. 5 Kindred) for getarg(0) points.
L_BondTier:
	for (.@t = 4; .@t > 0; .@t--) {
		if (getarg(0) >= .bt_need[.@t])
			return .@t + 1;
	}
	return 1;

// A tier's max HP/SP bonus on companion getarg(0) (an index), tier getarg(1):
// 2% at Companion and Trusted, 4% Sworn, 6% Kindred. Six minutes, renewed every minute.
L_BondBuff:
	setarray .@pc, 0, 0, 2, 2, 4, 6;
	.@v = .@pc[getarg(1)];
	if (!.@v)
		return;
	bonus_script "bonus bMaxHPrate," + .@v + "; bonus bMaxSPrate," + .@v + ";", 360, BSF_FORCE_REPLACE, 0, EFST_BLANK, @cl_cid[getarg(0)];
	return;

L_PairBond:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@j = getarg(1);
	.@t = modstore_get("char", callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@i], @cl_cid[.@j]), 0);
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 7 || @cl_ar[.@j] == 7)
		.@t = .@t * 3 / 2;
	if (@cl_ar[.@i] == 4 && @cl_hurt_aid == @cl_aid[.@j] && gettimetick(2) - @cl_hurt_t < 300)
		.@t += 5;
	return .@t;

// The store key for two companions' talks: "r.c<lower char id>_<higher>".
// (Plain numbers in: min()/max() given an array element read the whole rest
// of the array, which before 2.3.2 put pairs under the wrong key.)
L_PairKey:
	.@x = getarg(0);
	.@y = getarg(1);
	return "r.c" + min(.@x, .@y) + "_" + max(.@x, .@y);

// The emote for companion getarg(0) sitting by getarg(1) (-1 you): a smile for
// friends (6 talks, or a bond of 12 with you), a heart for close friends (20
// talks), a big heart for a bond of 30 with you.
L_BondEmote:
	.@i = getarg(0);
	.@f = getarg(1);
	if (.@f < 0) {
		.@b = callsub(L_PlayerBond, .@i);
		if (.@b >= 30)
			return ET_BIGTHROB;
		if (.@b >= 12)
			return ET_SMILE;
		return 0;
	}
	.@t = modstore_get("char", callsub(L_PairKey, @cl_cid[.@i], @cl_cid[.@f]), 0);
	if (.@t >= 20)
		return ET_THROB;
	if (.@t >= 6)
		return ET_SMILE;
	return 0;

// Speak over a unit's head, as players do. Skips one that left or fell.
L_SayAid:
	if (!unitexists(getarg(0)) || getd("@cl_ph_" + getarg(0)) == 1)
		return;
	unittalk getarg(0), getarg(1) + " : " + getarg(2);
	return;

L_EmoteIdx:
	callsub L_EmoteAid, @cl_aid[getarg(0)], getarg(1);
	return;

// An emote, at most one per companion every 12 s and one on screen every 2.5 s.
L_EmoteAid:
	if (!.emotes || !unitexists(getarg(0)))
		return;
	.@now = gettimetick(0);
	if (.@now < @cl_eall || .@now < getd("@cl_ecd_" + getarg(0)))
		return;
	setd "@cl_ecd_" + getarg(0), .@now + 12000;
	@cl_eall = .@now + 2500;
	emotion getarg(1), getarg(0);
	return;

// town / field / dungeon / "" (indoors, castles, arenas: say nothing).
L_MapKind:
	.@m$ = getarg(0);
	if (.@m$ == "")
		return "";	// between maps (getmapflag on "" returns nothing and broke the tick, 3.1.0)
	// Towns by name first: Morroc lacks the town flag in pre-renewal.
	setarray .@towns$, "prontera", "izlude", "geffen", "payon", "morocc", "alberta", "aldebaran", "yuno", "comodo", "umbala", "amatsu", "gonryun", "louyang", "ayothaya", "jawaii", "xmas", "niflheim", "einbroch", "einbech", "lighthalzen", "hugel", "rachel", "veins", "moscovia", "brasilis", "manuk", "splendide", "dewata", "malangdo", "malaya", "eclage", "mora", "dicastes01";
	if (inarray(.@towns$, .@m$) >= 0 || getmapflag(.@m$, MF_TOWN))
		return "town";
	if (compare(.@m$, "fild"))
		return "field";
	if (compare(.@m$, "sphinx"))
		return "dungeon";
	if (compare(.@m$, "_in") || compare(.@m$, "in_") || compare(.@m$, "cas0") || compare(.@m$, "pvp") || compare(.@m$, "job") || compare(.@m$, "que_") || compare(.@m$, "_vs"))
		return "";
	return "dungeon";
}


// Campfire nights (3.0.0): the bonfire. Never placed itself: L_CampStart makes a
// copy next to you with duplicate_dynamic (only you see it), and puts it out with
// unloadnpc. Clicking it: sit a while, or put it out. Sprite 19420 is our own id for
// the client's bonfire (System/jobname.lub): this client has no row for 4_BONFIRE's id.
// Putting it out only marks it (@cl_cout): unloading this NPC while its own dialog was
// still open left you "talking" to an NPC that no longer existed, and no other NPC
// would talk to you until a relog. The camp tick puts it out once the dialog is shut.
-	script	CampFire	19420,{
	mes "The fire crackles warmly. Your companions are gathered around it.";
	if (select("Sit a while longer:Put out the fire") == 2)
		@cl_cout = 1;
	close;
}

// Warp Portal (3.3.0): never placed itself: L_WarpOpen makes a copy next to you with
// duplicate_dynamic (only you see it). The copy keeps this header's touch area: 0,0 =
// its own cell, like the real skill. Clicking it is a "talk", which runs this script
// from the top from anywhere on screen: then you're walked onto the cell instead
// (3.3.3: before, a click warped you from where you stood), and the step takes you
// through. The talk cursor over it is the client's for every NPC (roBrowser TYPE_NPC);
// a mod can't change it. Companions are
// players too, so they can touch it: only the one whose @cl_wp$ names this copy goes.
// It never unloads itself (rAthena refuses a self-unload): the tick closes it.
-	script	CpoWarpPortal	111,0,0,{
OnTouch:
	if (@cl_wp$ != strnpcinfo(3) || @cl_wpdm$ == "")
		end;
	getmapxy(.@pm$, .@px, .@py, BL_PC);
	getmapxy(.@nm$, .@nx, .@ny, BL_NPC);
	if (.@px != .@nx || .@py != .@ny) {
		unitwalk getcharid(3), .@nx, .@ny;	// a click from afar: walk in
		end;
	}
	@cl_wpend = 0;	// the tick closes it after you
	warp @cl_wpdm$, @cl_wpdx, @cl_wpdy;
	end;
}
